Tôi sẽ hướng dẫn bạn chuyển đổi hệ thống POS từ HTML/CSS/JS sang JSX và SCSS một cách có hệ thống!

📦 Cài đặt dependencies
npm install react react-dom
npm install -D sass
npm install @hookform/react-hook-form
npm install react-hot-toast  # cho notifications
npm install lucide-react     # cho icons

    background: $gray-100;
  }

  @if $breakpoint == mobile {
    @media (max-width: $mobile) {
      @content;
    }
  }
  @if $breakpoint == tablet {
    @media (max-width: $tablet) {
      @content;
    }
  }
  @if $breakpoint == desktop {
    @media (min-width: $desktop) {
      @content;
    }
  }
}

🔧 Context & State Management
src/context/POSContext.jsx
import React, { createContext, useContext, useReducer } from 'react';

const POSContext = createContext();

const initialState = {
  currentTable: null,
  currentOrder: [],
  currentOrderType: 'dine_in',
  currentFloor: 1,
  currentCategory: 'all',
  tables: {
    1: [
      { code: 'T01', capacity: 2, status: 'available', customerName: '', phone: '', guestCount: 0 },
      { code: 'T02', capacity: 4, status: 'occupied', customerName: 'Nguyễn Văn A', phone: '0901234567', guestCount: 3 },
      // ... more tables
    ],
    // ... other floors
  },
  tableOrders: {},
  printers: {
    'kitchen-1': {
      id: 'kitchen-1',
      name: 'Máy in bếp chính',
      ip: '192.168.1.101',
      type: 'thermal',
      location: 'kitchen',
      status: 'online',
    },
    // ... more printers
  },
  printQueue: [],
};

function posReducer(state, action) {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'SELECT_TABLE':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentTable: action.payload,
        currentOrder: state.tableOrders[action.payload.code] || [],
      };

    case 'ADD_TO_ORDER':
      const newOrder = [...state.currentOrder, action.payload];
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: newOrder,
      };

    case 'UPDATE_ORDER_ITEM':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: state.currentOrder.map(item =>
          item.id === action.payload.id ? { ...item, ...action.payload.updates } : item
        ),
      };

    case 'REMOVE_ORDER_ITEM':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: state.currentOrder.filter(item => item.id !== action.payload),
      };

    case 'SAVE_ORDER':
      const tableCode = state.currentTable?.code;
      if (!tableCode) return state;

      return {
        ...state,
        tableOrders: {
          ...state.tableOrders,
          [tableCode]: [...state.currentOrder],
        },
      };

    case 'CLEAR_ORDER':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: [],
      };

    case 'UPDATE_TABLE_STATUS':
      const { tableCode: code, status, customerInfo } = action.payload;
      const updatedTables = { ...state.tables };
      
      Object.keys(updatedTables).forEach(floor => {
        const tableIndex = updatedTables[floor].findIndex(t => t.code === code);
        if (tableIndex !== -1) {
          updatedTables[floor][tableIndex] = {
            ...updatedTables[floor][tableIndex],
            status,
            ...customerInfo,
          };
        }
      });

      return {
        ...state,
        tables: updatedTables,
      };

    case 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE':
      return {
        ...state,
        printQueue: [...state.printQueue, action.payload],
      };

    case 'UPDATE_PRINTER_STATUS':
      return {
        ...state,
        printers: {
          ...state.printers,
          [action.payload.id]: {
            ...state.printers[action.payload.id],
            ...action.payload.updates,
          },
        },
      };

    default:
      return state;
  }
}

export function POSProvider({ children }) {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(posReducer, initialState);

  return (
    <POSContext.Provider value={{ state, dispatch }}>
      {children}
    </POSContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function usePOS() {
  const context = useContext(POSContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('usePOS must be used within a POSProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

🧩 Component Examples
src/components/Common/Button.jsx
import React from 'react';
import './Button.scss';

const Button = ({ 
  variant = 'primary', 
  size = 'md', 
  children, 
  className = '', 
  disabled = false,
  onClick,
  ...props 
}) => {
  const baseClass = 'btn';
  const variantClass = `btn--${variant}`;
  const sizeClass = `btn--${size}`;
  
  return (
    <button
      className={`${baseClass} ${variantClass} ${sizeClass} ${className}`}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onClick}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

export default Button;

src/components/Tables/TableItem.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { MoreVertical } from 'lucide-react';
import './TableItem.scss';

const TableItem = ({ 
  table, 
  isSelected, 
  onSelect, 
  onShowActions 
}) => {
  const handleClick = () => {
    onSelect(table);
  };

  const handleMenuClick = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onShowActions(e, table.code);
  };

  return (
    <div className="table-item-container">
      <div 
        className={`table-item table-item--${table.status} ${isSelected ? 'table-item--selected' : ''}`}
        onClick={handleClick}
      >
        <div className={`table-status-indicator table-status-indicator--${table.status}`} />
        
        <button 
          className="table-menu-btn"
          onClick={handleMenuClick}
          title="Tùy chọn bàn"
        >
          <MoreVertical size={12} />
        </button>
        
        <div className="table-code">{table.code}</div>
        <div className="table-capacity">{table.capacity} chỗ</div>
        
        {table.customerName && (
          <div className="table-customer-info">{table.customerName}</div>
        )}
        
        {table.guestCount > 0 && (
          <div className="table-customer-info">{table.guestCount} khách</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableItem;

src/hooks/useOrder.js
import { usePOS } from '../context/POSContext';
import { formatPrice } from '../utils/formatters';

export function useOrder() {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();

  const addToOrder = (menuItem, options) => {
    const orderItem = {
      id: Date.now(),
      menuItemId: menuItem.id,
      name: menuItem.name,
      price: menuItem.price,
      quantity: options.quantity,
      cookingOption: options.cookingOption,
      unit: options.unit,
      note: options.note,
      total: menuItem.price * options.quantity,
      isNew: true,
      isExisting: false,
      addedAt: new Date(),
    };

    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_ORDER', payload: orderItem });
  };

  const updateOrderItem = (itemId, updates) => {
    dispatch({ 
      type: 'UPDATE_ORDER_ITEM', 
      payload: { id: itemId, updates } 
    });
  };

  const removeOrderItem = (itemId) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_ORDER_ITEM', payload: itemId });
  };

  const changeQuantity = (itemId, change) => {
    const item = state.currentOrder.find(item => item.id === itemId);
    if (item) {
      const newQuantity = Math.max(1, item.quantity + change);
      const newTotal = item.price * newQuantity;
      
      updateOrderItem(itemId, { 
        quantity: newQuantity, 
        total: newTotal 
      });
    }
  };

  const calculateOrderSummary = () => {
    const subtotal = state.currentOrder.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
    const discount = 0;
    const tax = subtotal * 0.1;
    const service = subtotal * 0.05;
    const total = subtotal - discount + tax + service;

    return {
      subtotal,
      discount,
      tax,
      service,
      total,
      formatted: {
        subtotal: formatPrice(subtotal),
        discount: formatPrice(discount),
        tax: formatPrice(tax),
        service: formatPrice(service),
        total: formatPrice(total),
      }
    };
  };

  const saveOrder = () => {
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_ORDER' });
    
    // Mark all items as existing (no longer new)
    state.currentOrder.forEach(item => {
      updateOrderItem(item.id, { isNew: false, isExisting: true });
    });
  };

  const clearOrder = () => {
    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_ORDER' });
  };

  return {
    currentOrder: state.currentOrder,
    addToOrder,
    updateOrderItem,
    removeOrderItem,
    changeQuantity,
    calculateOrderSummary,
    saveOrder,
    clearOrder,
  };
}

src/components/Order/OrderPanel.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Trash2, Save, Printer } from 'lucide-react';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import OrderItem from './OrderItem';
import OrderSummary from './OrderSummary';
import './OrderPanel.scss';

const OrderPanel = ({ onShowPrintModal, onProcessPayment }) => {
  const { state } = usePOS();
  const { currentOrder, saveOrder, clearOrder } = useOrder();

  const handleClearOrder = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa toàn bộ đơn hàng?')) {
      clearOrder();
    }
  };

  const existingItems = currentOrder.filter(item => item.isExisting);
  const newItems = currentOrder.filter(item => item.isNew);

  return (
    <div className="order-panel">
      <div className="order-header">
        <div className="order-info">
          <div className="table-info">
            {state.currentTable ? `Bàn ${state.currentTable.code}` : 'Chọn bàn'}
          </div>
          <div className="order-time">
            {new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN')}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="order-items">
        {currentOrder.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="order-empty">
            Chưa có món nào được chọn
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            {existingItems.length > 0 && (
              <div className="order-section">
                <div className="order-section-header order-section-header--existing">
                  📋 Món đã có ({existingItems.length})
                </div>
                {existingItems.map(item => (
                  <OrderItem key={item.id} item={item} variant="existing" />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {newItems.length > 0 && (
              <div className="order-section">
                <div className="order-section-header order-section-header--new">
                  ✨ Món mới thêm ({newItems.length})
                </div>
                {newItems.map(item => (
                  <OrderItem key={item.id} item={item} variant="new" />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <OrderSummary />

      <div className="action-buttons">
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleClearOrder}>
          <Trash2 size={16} />
          Xóa
        </Button>
        
        <Button variant="primary" onClick={saveOrder}>
          <Save size={16} />
          Lưu
        </Button>
        
        <Button variant="warning" onClick={onShowPrintModal}>
          <Printer size={16} />
          In đơn
        </Button>
        
        <Button variant="success" onClick={onProcessPayment}>
          Thanh toán
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default OrderPanel;

🚀 Main App Component
src/App.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';
import { POSProvider } from './context/POSContext';
import TableGrid from './components/Tables/TableGrid';
import MenuGrid from './components/Menu/MenuGrid';
import OrderPanel from './components/Order/OrderPanel';
import MenuItemModal from './components/Modals/MenuItemModal';
import PaymentModal from './components/Modals/PaymentModal';
import PrintModal from './components/Modals/PrintModal';
import './styles/globals.scss';

function App() {
  const [showMenuItemModal, setShowMenuItemModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPaymentModal, setShowPaymentModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPrintModal, setShowPrintModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedMenuItem, setSelectedMenuItem] = useState(null);

  const handleMenuItemClick = (menuItem) => {
    setSelectedMenuItem(menuItem);
    setShowMenuItemModal(true);
  };

  return (
    <POSProvider>
      <div className="pos-container">
        <div className="left-panel">
          <TableGrid />
        </div>

        <div className="center-panel">
          <MenuGrid onMenuItemClick={handleMenuItemClick} />
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <OrderPanel 
            onShowPrintModal={() => setShowPrintModal(true)}
            onProcessPayment={() => setShowPaymentModal(true)}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Modals */}
        <MenuItemModal
          isOpen={showMenuItemModal}
          onClose={() => setShowMenuItemModal(false)}
          menuItem={selectedMenuItem}
        />

        <PaymentModal
          isOpen={showPaymentModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPaymentModal(false)}
        />

        <PrintModal
          isOpen={showPrintModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPrintModal(false)}
        />

        <Toaster position="top-right" />
      </div>
    </POSProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

🔧 Utility Functions
src/utils/formatters.js
export const formatPrice = (price) => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'VND'
  }).format(price);
};

export const formatDateTime = (date) => {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleString('vi-VN');
};

export const formatTime = (date) => {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN');
};

🎯 Migration Tips
Từng bước một: Chuyển đổi từng component một, bắt đầu từ các component nhỏ nhất
Tái sử dụng logic: Chuyển JavaScript functions thành custom hooks
State management: Sử dụng Context API cho global state, useState cho local state
Styling: Chuyển đổi CSS classes thành SCSS modules hoặc styled-components
Event handling: Chuyển onclick thành onClick props
Form handling: Sử dụng react-hook-form cho forms phức tạp
Bạn muốn tôi hướng dẫn chi tiết phần nào trước? Component nào bạn muốn bắt đầu chuyển đổi?

cung cấp cho tôi toàn bộ file cần thiết cho trang POS

Tôi sẽ cung cấp toàn bộ file cần thiết cho trang POS React với JSX và SCSS!

{
  "name": "foodhub-pos",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "private": true,
  "dependencies": {

const POSContext = createContext();

const initialState = {

function posReducer(state, action) {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'SELECT_TABLE':
      const tableOrders = state.tableOrders[action.payload.code] || [];
      return {
        ...state,
        currentTable: action.payload,
        currentOrder: tableOrders.map(item => ({ ...item, isNew: false, isExisting: true })),
        currentOrderType: 'dine_in'
      };

    case 'SET_FLOOR':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentFloor: action.payload
      };

    case 'SET_CATEGORY':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentCategory: action.payload
      };

    case 'SET_SEARCH_TERM':
      return {
        ...state,
        searchTerm: action.payload
      };

    case 'ADD_TO_ORDER':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: [...state.currentOrder, action.payload]
      };

    case 'UPDATE_ORDER_ITEM':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: state.currentOrder.map(item =>
          item.id === action.payload.id ? { ...item, ...action.payload.updates } : item
        )
      };

    case 'REMOVE_ORDER_ITEM':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: state.currentOrder.filter(item => item.id !== action.payload)
      };

    case 'CLEAR_ORDER':
      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: []
      };

    case 'SAVE_ORDER':
      const tableCode = state.currentTable?.code;
      if (!tableCode) return state;

      // Mark all items as existing
      const updatedOrder = state.currentOrder.map(item => ({
        ...item,
        isNew: false,
        isExisting: true
      }));

      return {
        ...state,
        currentOrder: updatedOrder,
        tableOrders: {
          ...state.tableOrders,
          [tableCode]: [...updatedOrder]
        }
      };

    case 'UPDATE_TABLE':
      const { tableCode: code, updates } = action.payload;
      const updatedTables = { ...state.tables };
      
      Object.keys(updatedTables).forEach(floor => {
        const tableIndex = updatedTables[floor].findIndex(t => t.code === code);
        if (tableIndex !== -1) {
          updatedTables[floor][tableIndex] = {
            ...updatedTables[floor][tableIndex],
            ...updates
          };
        }
      });

      return {
        ...state,
        tables: updatedTables
      };

    case 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE':
      return {
        ...state,
        printQueue: [...state.printQueue, action.payload]
      };

    case 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM':
      return {
        ...state,
        printQueue: state.printQueue.map(item =>
          item.id === action.payload.id ? { ...item, ...action.payload.updates } : item
        )
      };

    case 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE':
      return {
        ...state,
        printQueue: state.printQueue.filter(item => item.id !== action.payload)
      };

    case 'CLEAR_PRINT_QUEUE':
      return {
        ...state,
        printQueue: []
      };

    case 'UPDATE_PRINTER':
      return {
        ...state,
        printers: {
          ...state.printers,
          [action.payload.id]: {
            ...state.printers[action.payload.id],
            ...action.payload.updates
          }
        }
      };

    default:
      return state;
  }
}

export function POSProvider({ children }) {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(posReducer, initialState);

  // Auto-update printer status simulation
  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      Object.values(state.printers).forEach(printer => {
        if (Math.random() < 0.05) { // 5% chance of status change
          const newStatus = printer.status === 'online' 
            ? (Math.random() < 0.7 ? 'online' : 'offline')
            : (Math.random() < 0.3 ? 'online' : 'offline');
          
          if (newStatus !== printer.status) {
            dispatch({
              type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
              payload: {
                id: printer.id,
                updates: { status: newStatus }
              }
            });
          }
        }
      });
    }, 5000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [state.printers]);

  return (
    <POSContext.Provider value={{ state, dispatch }}>
      {children}
    </POSContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function usePOS() {
  const context = useContext(POSContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('usePOS must be used within a POSProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

src/hooks/useOrder.js
import { usePOS } from '../context/POSContext';
import { formatPrice } from '../utils/formatters';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export function useOrder() {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();

  const addToOrder = (menuItem, options) => {
    const orderItem = {
      id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
      menuItemId: menuItem.id,
      name: menuItem.name,
      price: menuItem.price,
      quantity: options.quantity,
      cookingOption: options.cookingOption || 'Bình thường',
      unit: options.unit || 'Phần',
      note: options.note || '',
      total: menuItem.price * options.quantity,
      isNew: true,
      isExisting: false,
      addedAt: new Date()
    };

    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_ORDER', payload: orderItem });
    toast.success(`Đã thêm ${menuItem.name} vào đơn hàng`);
  };

  const updateOrderItem = (itemId, updates) => {
    dispatch({ 
      type: 'UPDATE_ORDER_ITEM', 
      payload: { id: itemId, updates } 
    });
  };

  const removeOrderItem = (itemId) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_ORDER_ITEM', payload: itemId });
    toast.success('Đã xóa món khỏi đơn hàng');
  };

  const changeQuantity = (itemId, change) => {
    const item = state.currentOrder.find(item => item.id === itemId);
    if (item) {
      const newQuantity = Math.max(1, item.quantity + change);
      const newTotal = item.price * newQuantity;
      
      updateOrderItem(itemId, { 
        quantity: newQuantity, 
        total: newTotal 
      });
    }
  };

  const calculateOrderSummary = () => {
    const subtotal = state.currentOrder.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
    const discount = 0;
    const tax = subtotal * 0.1;
    const service = subtotal * 0.05;
    const total = subtotal - discount + tax + service;

    return {
      subtotal,
      discount,
      tax,
      service,
      total,
      formatted: {
        subtotal: formatPrice(subtotal),
        discount: formatPrice(discount),
        tax: formatPrice(tax),
        service: formatPrice(service),
        total: formatPrice(total)
      }
    };
  };

  const saveOrder = () => {
    if (state.currentOrder.length === 0) {
      toast.error('Vui lòng chọn ít nhất một món!');
      return;
    }

    // Check for new items to auto-send
    const newItems = state.currentOrder.filter(item => item.isNew);
    
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_ORDER' });
    
    // Update table status if needed
    if (state.currentTable) {
      const table = findTableByCode(state.currentTable.code);
      if (table && table.status === 'available') {
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_TABLE',
          payload: {
            tableCode: state.currentTable.code,
            updates: { status: 'occupied' }
          }
        });
      }
    }

    if (newItems.length > 0) {
      autoSendToPrinters(newItems);
      toast.success(`Đã lưu đơn hàng và gửi ${newItems.length} món mới đến bếp/bar!`);
    } else {
      toast.success('Đã lưu đơn hàng thành công!');
    }
  };

  const clearOrder = () => {
    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_ORDER' });
    toast.success('Đã xóa toàn bộ đơn hàng');
  };

  const autoSendToPrinters = (newItems) => {
    // Separate items by category
    const kitchenItems = newItems.filter(item => {
      const menuItem = state.menuItems.find(m => m.id === item.menuItemId);
      return menuItem && ['appetizer', 'main', 'seafood', 'hotpot'].includes(menuItem.category);
    });
    
    const barItems = newItems.filter(item => {
      const menuItem = state.menuItems.find(m => m.id === item.menuItemId);
      return menuItem && ['drink', 'dessert'].includes(menuItem.category);
    });

    // Find available printers
    const kitchenPrinter = Object.values(state.printers).find(p => 
      p.location === 'kitchen' && p.status === 'online'
    );
    
    const barPrinter = Object.values(state.printers).find(p => 
      p.location === 'bar' && p.status === 'online'
    );

    let sentMessages = [];
    let warningMessages = [];

    // Send kitchen items
    if (kitchenItems.length > 0) {
      if (kitchenPrinter) {
        const queueItem = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          type: 'kitchen',
          printer: kitchenPrinter,
          table: state.currentTable?.code || 'Mang về',
          order: kitchenItems,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          status: 'printing'
        };
        
        dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: queueItem });
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
          payload: {
            id: kitchenPrinter.id,
            updates: { status: 'busy', lastUsed: new Date() }
          }
        });

        // Simulate print completion
        setTimeout(() => {
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
            payload: { id: queueItem.id, updates: { status: 'completed' } }
          });
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
            payload: {
              id: kitchenPrinter.id,
              updates: { status: 'online' }
            }
          });
        }, 2000);

        sentMessages.push(`Bếp: ${kitchenItems.length} món`);
      } else {
        warningMessages.push(`Bếp: ${kitchenItems.length} món (máy in không khả dụng)`);
      }
    }

    // Send bar items
    if (barItems.length > 0) {
      if (barPrinter) {
        const queueItem = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random() + 1,
          type: 'bar',
          printer: barPrinter,
          table: state.currentTable?.code || 'Mang về',
          order: barItems,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          status: 'printing'
        };
        
        dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: queueItem });
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
          payload: {
            id: barPrinter.id,
            updates: { status: 'busy', lastUsed: new Date() }
          }
        });

        // Simulate print completion
        setTimeout(() => {
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
            payload: { id: queueItem.id, updates: { status: 'completed' } }
          });
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
            payload: {
              id: barPrinter.id,
              updates: { status: 'online' }
            }
          });
        }, 2500);

        sentMessages.push(`Bar: ${barItems.length} món`);
      } else {
        warningMessages.push(`Bar: ${barItems.length} món (máy in không khả dụng)`);
      }
    }

    // Show notifications
    if (sentMessages.length > 0) {
      toast.success(`🖨️ Đã gửi đơn đến: ${sentMessages.join(' • ')}`);
    }
    
    if (warningMessages.length > 0) {
      toast.error(`⚠️ Không thể gửi: ${warningMessages.join(' • ')}`);
    }
  };

  const findTableByCode = (code) => {
    return Object.values(state.tables).flat().find(table => table.code === code);
  };

  return {
    currentOrder: state.currentOrder,
    addToOrder,
    updateOrderItem,
    removeOrderItem,
    changeQuantity,
    calculateOrderSummary,
    saveOrder,
    clearOrder
  };
}

src/hooks/useTables.js
import { usePOS } from '../context/POSContext';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export function useTables() {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();

  const selectTable = (table) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'SELECT_TABLE', payload: table });
  };

  const selectFloor = (floor) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'SET_FLOOR', payload: floor });
  };

  const updateTable = (tableCode, updates) => {
    dispatch({
      type: 'UPDATE_TABLE',
      payload: { tableCode, updates }
    });
  };

  const changeTableStatus = (tableCode, status, customerInfo = {}) => {
    const updates = { status, ...customerInfo };
    
    if (status === 'available') {
      // Clear customer info when table becomes available
      updates.customerName = '';
      updates.phone = '';
      updates.guestCount = 0;
      updates.reservationTime = '';
      updates.checkinTime = '';
      updates.note = '';
      
      // Clear orders for this table
      const newTableOrders = { ...state.tableOrders };
      delete newTableOrders[tableCode];
      // Note: This would need a separate action in a real implementation
    }

    updateTable(tableCode, updates);
    toast.success(`Đã cập nhật trạng thái bàn ${tableCode}`);
  };

  const cleanTable = (tableCode) => {
    changeTableStatus(tableCode, 'available');
    
    // Clear current order if this is the selected table
    if (state.currentTable?.code === tableCode) {
      dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_ORDER' });
    }
    
    toast.success(`Đã dọn bàn ${tableCode}`);
  };

  const moveTable = (sourceTableCode, targetTableCode) => {
    const sourceTable = findTableByCode(sourceTableCode);
    const targetTable = findTableByCode(targetTableCode);
    
    if (!sourceTable || !targetTable) {
      toast.error('Không tìm thấy bàn!');
      return;
    }

    if (targetTable.status !== 'available') {
      toast.error('Bàn đích không trống!');
      return;
    }

    // Copy customer info to target table
    updateTable(targetTableCode, {
      customerName: sourceTable.customerName,
      phone: sourceTable.phone,
      guestCount: sourceTable.guestCount,
      reservationTime: sourceTable.reservationTime,
      note: sourceTable.note,
      status: sourceTable.status
    });

    // Clear source table
    changeTableStatus(sourceTableCode, 'available');

    // Move orders (would need additional logic in real implementation)
    
    toast.success(`Đã chuyển từ bàn ${sourceTableCode} sang bàn ${targetTableCode}`);
  };

  const mergeTable = (sourceTableCode, targetTableCode) => {
    const sourceTable = findTableByCode(sourceTableCode);
    const targetTable = findTableByCode(targetTableCode);
    
    if (!sourceTable || !targetTable) {
      toast.error('Không tìm thấy bàn!');
      return;
    }

    const totalCapacity = sourceTable.capacity + targetTable.capacity;
    const customerInfo = sourceTable.customerName || targetTable.customerName;

    // Update target table
    updateTable(targetTableCode, {
      capacity: totalCapacity,
      status: 'occupied',
      customerName: customerInfo,
      guestCount: Math.max(sourceTable.guestCount, targetTable.guestCount),
      note: `Gộp từ: ${sourceTableCode}, ${targetTableCode}`
    });

    // Clear source table
    changeTableStatus(sourceTableCode, 'available');

    toast.success(`Đã gộp bàn ${sourceTableCode} vào bàn ${targetTableCode} (${totalCapacity} chỗ)`);
  };

  const makeReservation = (reservationData) => {
    const { customerName, customerPhone, guestCount, reservationTime, note } = reservationData;

    // Find suitable available table
    const availableTables = Object.values(state.tables).flat().filter(table => 
      table.status === 'available' && table.capacity >= guestCount
    );

    if (availableTables.length === 0) {
      toast.error('Không có bàn trống phù hợp!');
      return false;
    }

    // Choose the smallest suitable table
    const suitableTable = availableTables.reduce((best, current) => 
      current.capacity < best.capacity ? current : best
    );

    updateTable(suitableTable.code, {
      status: 'reserved',
      customerName,
      phone: customerPhone,
      guestCount,
      reservationTime,
      note
    });

    toast.success(`Đã đặt bàn ${suitableTable.code} cho ${customerName} (${guestCount} khách)`);
    return true;
  };

  const findTableByCode = (code) => {
    return Object.values(state.tables).flat().find(table => table.code === code);
  };

  const getTablesByFloor = (floor) => {
    return state.tables[floor] || [];
  };

  const getCurrentFloorTables = () => {
    return getTablesByFloor(state.currentFloor);
  };

  return {
    tables: state.tables,
    currentFloor: state.currentFloor,
    currentTable: state.currentTable,
    selectTable,
    selectFloor,
    updateTable,
    changeTableStatus,
    cleanTable,
    moveTable,
    mergeTable,
    makeReservation,
    findTableByCode,
    getTablesByFloor,
    getCurrentFloorTables
  };
}

const Button = ({ 
  variant = 'primary', 
  size = 'md', 
  children,

}) => {

      type={type}
      className={`${baseClass} ${variantClass} ${sizeClass} ${className}`}

      onClick={onClick}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};

export default Button;

src/components/Common/Modal.jsx
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import Button from './Button';
import './Modal.scss';

const Modal = ({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  title, 
  children, 
  size = 'md',
  showCloseButton = true,
  className = ''
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    } else {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
    }

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = 'unset';
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleEscape = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && isOpen) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleOverlayClick = (e) => {
    if (e.target === e.currentTarget) {
      onClose();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`modal ${isOpen ? 'show' : ''}`} onClick={handleOverlayClick}>
      <div className={`modal-content modal-content--${size} ${className}`}>
        {(title || showCloseButton) && (
          <div className="modal-header">
            {title && <h3 className="modal-title">{title}</h3>}
            {showCloseButton && (
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                size="sm"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="modal-close-btn"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        
        <div className="modal-body">
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Modal;

src/components/Tables/TableGrid.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Printer } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import TableItem from './TableItem';
import TableActions from './TableActions';
import ReservationModal from '../Modals/ReservationModal';
import './TableGrid.scss';

const TableGrid = () => {
  const { 
    currentFloor, 
    selectFloor, 
    getCurrentFloorTables, 
    currentTable 
  } = useTables();
  
  const [showReservationModal, setShowReservationModal] = useState(false);
  const [showTableActions, setShowTableActions] = useState(null);
  const [tableActionsPosition, setTableActionsPosition] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const tables = getCurrentFloorTables();

  const handleFloorChange = (e) => {
    selectFloor(parseInt(e.target.value));
  };

  const handleShowTableActions = (e, tableCode) => {
    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    setTableActionsPosition({
      x: rect.right,
      y: rect.bottom
    });
    setShowTableActions(tableCode);
  };

  const handleCloseTableActions = () => {
    setShowTableActions(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="table-grid-container">
      <div className="table-grid-header">
        <div className="nav-tabs">
          <button className="nav-tab active">Bàn ăn</button>
          <button className="nav-tab">Giao hàng</button>
          <button className="nav-tab">Mang về</button>
        </div>
        
        <div className="table-controls">
          <select 
            className="floor-select" 
            value={currentFloor} 
            onChange={handleFloorChange}
          >
            <option value={1}>Tầng 1</option>
            <option value={2}>Tầng 2</option>
            <option value={3}>Tầng 3</option>
            <option value={4}>Tầng 4</option>
            <option value={5}>Tầng 5</option>
          </select>
          
          <Button 
            variant="secondary" 
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setShowReservationModal(true)}
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            Đặt bàn
          </Button>
          
          <Button 
            variant="primary" 
            size="sm"
          >
            <Printer size={16} />
            Hàng đợi in
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="tables-grid">
        {tables.map(table => (
          <TableItem
            key={table.code}
            table={table}
            isSelected={currentTable?.code === table.code}
            onShowActions={handleShowTableActions}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      {showTableActions && (
        <TableActions
          tableCode={showTableActions}
          position={tableActionsPosition}
          onClose={handleCloseTableActions}
        />
      )}

      <ReservationModal
        isOpen={showReservationModal}
        onClose={() => setShowReservationModal(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableGrid;

src/components/Tables/TableItem.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { MoreVertical } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import './TableItem.scss';

const TableItem = ({ table, isSelected, onShowActions }) => {
  const { selectTable } = useTables();

  const handleClick = () => {
    selectTable(table);
  };

  const handleMenuClick = (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    onShowActions(e, table.code);
  };

  return (
    <div className="table-item-container">
      <div 
        className={`table-item table-item--${table.status} ${isSelected ? 'table-item--selected' : ''}`}
        onClick={handleClick}
      >
        <div className={`table-status-indicator table-status-indicator--${table.status}`} />
        
        <button 
          className="table-menu-btn"
          onClick={handleMenuClick}
          title="Tùy chọn bàn"
        >
          <MoreVertical size={12} />
        </button>
        
        <div className="table-code">{table.code}</div>
        <div className="table-capacity">{table.capacity} chỗ</div>
        
        {table.customerName && (
          <div className="table-customer-info">{table.customerName}</div>
        )}
        
        {table.guestCount > 0 && (
          <div className="table-customer-info">{table.guestCount} khách</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableItem;

src/components/Tables/TableActions.jsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  Info, 
  Users, 
  Link, 
  Scissors, 
  RotateCcw, 
  CheckCircle, 
  UserCheck, 
  Calendar, 
  Trash2 
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import './TableActions.scss';

const TableActions = ({ tableCode, position, onClose }) => {
  const { changeTableStatus, cleanTable } = useTables();
  const actionsRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleClickOutside = (event) => {
      if (actionsRef.current && !actionsRef.current.contains(event.target)) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, [onClose]);

  const handleAction = (action) => {
    switch (action) {
      case 'info':
        // Handle show table info
        break;
      case 'merge':
        // Handle merge table
        break;
      case 'split':
        // Handle split table
        break;
      case 'move':
        // Handle move table
        break;
      case 'available':
        changeTableStatus(tableCode, 'available');
        break;
      case 'occupied':
        changeTableStatus(tableCode, 'occupied');
        break;
      case 'reserved':
        changeTableStatus(tableCode, 'reserved');
        break;
      case 'clean':
        cleanTable(tableCode);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div 
      ref={actionsRef}
      className="table-actions"
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        top: position.y,
        left: position.x,
        zIndex: 1000
      }}
    >
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('info')}>
        <Info size={14} />
        <span>Thông tin bàn</span>
      </button>
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('merge')}>
        <Link size={14} />
        <span>Gộp bàn</span>
      </button>
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('split')}>
        <Scissors size={14} />
        <span>Tách bàn</span>
      </button>
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('move')}>
        <RotateCcw size={14} />
        <span>Chuyển bàn</span>
      </button>
      
      <div className="table-actions-divider" />
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('available')}>
        <CheckCircle size={14} />
        <span>Đánh dấu trống</span>
      </button>
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('occupied')}>
        <Users size={14} />
        <span>Đánh dấu có khách</span>
      </button>
      
      <button className="table-action-btn" onClick={() => handleAction('reserved')}>
        <Calendar size={14} />
        <span>Đánh dấu đã đặt</span>
      </button>
      
      <div className="table-actions-divider" />
      
      <button className="table-action-btn table-action-btn--danger" onClick={() => handleAction('clean')}>
        <Trash2 size={14} />
        <span>Dọn bàn</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableActions;

🍽️ Menu Components
src/components/Menu/MenuGrid.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { MENU_CATEGORIES } from '../../utils/constants';
import MenuItem from './MenuItem';
import CategoryTabs from './CategoryTabs';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import './MenuGrid.scss';

const MenuGrid = ({ onMenuItemClick }) => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const { menuItems, currentCategory, searchTerm } = state;

  const handleSearchChange = (e) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'SET_SEARCH_TERM', payload: e.target.value });
  };

  const filteredItems = menuItems.filter(item => {
    const matchesCategory = currentCategory === 'all' || item.category === currentCategory;
    const matchesSearch = !searchTerm || 
      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.description.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
    
    return matchesCategory && matchesSearch;
  });

  return (
    <div className="menu-grid-container">
      <div className="menu-header">
        <h2 className="menu-title">Thực đơn</h2>
        <div className="search-box">
          <div className="search-input-container">
            <Search size={16} className="search-icon" />
            <Input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Tìm kiếm món ăn..."
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={handleSearchChange}
              className="search-input"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <CategoryTabs />

      <div className="menu-grid">
        {filteredItems.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="menu-empty">
            <p>Không tìm thấy món ăn nào</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          filteredItems.map(item => (
            <MenuItem
              key={item.id}
              item={item}
              onClick={() => onMenuItemClick(item)}
            />
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MenuGrid;

src/components/Menu/CategoryTabs.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { MENU_CATEGORIES } from '../../utils/constants';
import './CategoryTabs.scss';

const CategoryTabs = () => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const { currentCategory } = state;

  const handleCategorySelect = (categoryId) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'SET_CATEGORY', payload: categoryId });
  };

  return (
    <div className="category-tabs-container">
      <div className="category-tabs">
        {MENU_CATEGORIES.map(category => (
          <button
            key={category.id}
            className={`category-tab ${currentCategory === category.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => handleCategorySelect(category.id)}
          >
            {category.name}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CategoryTabs;

📋 Order Components
src/components/Order/OrderPanel.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Trash2, Save, Printer, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import OrderItem from './OrderItem';
import OrderSummary from './OrderSummary';
import './OrderPanel.scss';

const OrderPanel = ({ onShowPrintModal, onProcessPayment }) => {
  const { state } = usePOS();
  const { currentOrder, saveOrder, clearOrder } = useOrder();

  const handleClearOrder = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa toàn bộ đơn hàng?')) {
      clearOrder();
    }
  };

  const handlePrintAll = () => {
    if (currentOrder.length === 0) {
      alert('Vui lòng chọn ít nhất một món để in!');
      return;
    }
    onShowPrintModal();
  };

  const existingItems = currentOrder.filter(item => item.isExisting);
  const newItems = currentOrder.filter(item => item.isNew);

  return (
    <div className="order-panel">
      <div className="order-header">
        <div className="order-info">
          <div className="table-info">
            {state.currentTable ? `Bàn ${state.currentTable.code} (${state.currentTable.capacity} chỗ)` : 'Chọn bàn'}
          </div>
          <div className="order-time">
            {new Date().toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN')}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="order-items">
        {currentOrder.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="order-empty">
            <p>Chưa có món nào được chọn</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            {existingItems.length > 0 && (
              <div className="order-section">
                <div className="order-section-header order-section-header--existing">
                  <span className="section-icon">📋</span>
                  <span>Món đã có ({existingItems.length})</span>
                </div>
                {existingItems.map(item => (
                  <OrderItem key={item.id} item={item} variant="existing" />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}

            {newItems.length > 0 && (
              <div className="order-section">
                <div className="order-section-header order-section-header--new">
                  <span className="section-icon">✨</span>
                  <span>Món mới thêm ({newItems.length})</span>
                </div>
                {newItems.map(item => (
                  <OrderItem key={item.id} item={item} variant="new" />
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <OrderSummary />

      <div className="action-buttons">
        <Button 
          variant="secondary" 
          onClick={handleClearOrder}
          disabled={currentOrder.length === 0}
        >
          <Trash2 size={16} />
          Xóa
        </Button>
        
        <Button 
          variant="primary" 
          onClick={saveOrder}
          disabled={currentOrder.length === 0}
        >
          <Save size={16} />
          Lưu
        </Button>
        
        <Button 
          variant="warning" 
          onClick={handlePrintAll}
          disabled={currentOrder.length === 0}
        >
          <Printer size={16} />
          🖨️ In tổng
        </Button>
        
        <Button 
          variant="primary" 
          onClick={onShowPrintModal}
          disabled={currentOrder.length === 0}
        >
          <Printer size={16} />
          In đơn
        </Button>
        
        <Button 
          variant="success" 
          onClick={onProcessPayment}
          disabled={currentOrder.length === 0}
        >
          <CreditCard size={16} />
          Thanh toán
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default OrderPanel;

src/components/Order/OrderItem.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Minus, Plus, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import { formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import './OrderItem.scss';

const OrderItem = ({ item, variant = 'existing' }) => {
  const { changeQuantity, removeOrderItem } = useOrder();

  const handleQuantityChange = (change) => {
    changeQuantity(item.id, change);
  };

  const handleRemove = () => {
    removeOrderItem(item.id);
  };

  return (
    <div className={`order-item order-item--${variant}`}>
      <div className="order-item-info">
        <div className="order-item-name">
          {item.name}
          {variant === 'new' && (
            <span className="new-badge">MỚI</span>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="order-item-options">
          {item.cookingOption !== 'Bình thường' && (
            <span>{item.cookingOption}</span>
          )}
          <span>{item.unit}</span>
          {item.note && <span>{item.note}</span>}
          {variant === 'new' && item.addedAt && (
            <span>{item.addedAt.toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN')}</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="quantity-controls">
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(-1)}
          className="qty-btn"
        >
          <Minus size={12} />
        </Button>
        
        <span className="quantity">{item.quantity}</span>
        
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(1)}
          className="qty-btn"
        >
          <Plus size={12} />
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="order-item-price">
        {formatPrice(item.total)}
      </div>

      <Button
        variant="error"
        size="sm"
        onClick={handleRemove}
        className="remove-btn"
      >
        <X size={12} />
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
};

export default OrderItem;

src/components/Order/OrderSummary.jsx
import React from 'react';

const OrderSummary = () => {

  const summary = calculateOrderSummary();

  return (
    <div className="order-summary">
      <div className="summary-row">
        <span>Tạm tính:</span>
        <span>{summary.formatted.subtotal}</span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="summary-row">
        <span>Giảm giá:</span>
        <span>{summary.formatted.discount}</span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="summary-row">
        <span>Thuế VAT (10%):</span>
        <span>{summary.formatted.tax}</span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="summary-row">
        <span>Phí phục vụ (5%):</span>
        <span>{summary.formatted.service}</span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="summary-row summary-row--total">
        <span>Tổng cộng:</span>
        <span>{summary.formatted.total}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

    color: $primary-color;
    border-top: 1px solid $gray-200;
    padding-top: $spacing-sm;
    margin-top: $spacing-md;
  }
}

🎯 Modal Components
src/components/Modals/MenuItemModal.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import './MenuItemModal.scss';

const MenuItemModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, menuItem }) => {
  const { addToOrder } = useOrder();
  const { register, handleSubmit, setValue, watch, reset } = useForm({
    defaultValues: {
      quantity: 1,
      cookingOption: 'Bình thường',
      unit: 'Phần',
      note: ''
    }
  });

  const quantity = watch('quantity');
  const [selectedCookingOption, setSelectedCookingOption] = useState('Bình thường');
  const [selectedUnit, setSelectedUnit] = useState('Phần');

  const cookingOptions = ['Bình thường', 'Ít cay', 'Cay vừa', 'Cay nhiều'];
  const unitOptions = ['Phần', 'Kg', 'Suất'];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && menuItem) {
      reset({
        quantity: 1,
        cookingOption: 'Bình thường',
        unit: 'Phần',
        note: ''
      });
      setSelectedCookingOption('Bình thường');
      setSelectedUnit('Phần');
    }
  }, [isOpen, menuItem, reset]);

  const handleQuantityChange = (change) => {
    const newQuantity = Math.max(1, quantity + change);
    setValue('quantity', newQuantity);
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (!menuItem) return;

    addToOrder(menuItem, {
      ...data,
      cookingOption: selectedCookingOption,
      unit: selectedUnit
    });

    onClose();
  };

  if (!menuItem) return null;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={menuItem.name}
      size="md"
    >
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="menu-item-form">
        <div className="form-group">
          <label className="form-label">Cách chế biến:</label>
          <div className="options-grid">
            {cookingOptions.map(option => (
              <button
                key={option}
                type="button"
                className={`option-btn ${selectedCookingOption === option ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCookingOption(option)}
              >
                {option}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="form-group">
          <label className="form-label">Đơn vị tính:</label>
          <div className="options-grid">
            {unitOptions.map(option => (
              <button
                key={option}
                type="button"
                className={`option-btn ${selectedUnit === option ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setSelectedUnit(option)}
              >
                {option}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="form-group">
          <label className="form-label">Số lượng:</label>
          <div className="quantity-input">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(-1)}
            >
              <Minus size={16} />
            </Button>
            
            <input
              type="number"
              {...register('quantity', { min: 1 })}
              className="quantity-field"
              min="1"
            />
            
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => handleQuantityChange(1)}
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <Input
          label="Ghi chú:"
          {...register('note')}
          placeholder="Ghi chú đặc biệt..."
        />

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="flex-1">
            Thêm vào đơn
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default MenuItemModal;

src/components/Modals/ReservationModal.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';

const ReservationModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { makeReservation } = useTables();
  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm();

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    const success = makeReservation(data);
    if (success) {
      reset();
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    reset();
    onClose();
  };

  // Set default time to 1 hour from now
  const getDefaultTime = () => {
    const now = new Date();
    now.setHours(now.getHours() + 1);
    return now.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={handleClose}
      title="Đặt bàn"
      size="md"
    >
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
        <Input
          label="Tên khách hàng:"
          {...register('customerName', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tên khách hàng' })}
          error={errors.customerName?.message}
          placeholder="Nhập tên khách hàng"
        />

        <Input
          label="Số điện thoại:"
          type="tel"
          {...register('customerPhone', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại' })}
          error={errors.customerPhone?.message}
          placeholder="Nhập số điện thoại"
        />

        <Input
          label="Số khách:"
          type="number"
          {...register('guestCount', { 
            required: 'Vui lòng nhập số khách',
            min: { value: 1, message: 'Số khách phải lớn hơn 0' }
          })}
          error={errors.guestCount?.message}
          placeholder="Số lượng khách"
          min="1"
        />

        <Input
          label="Thời gian đặt:"
          type="datetime-local"
          {...register('reservationTime', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn thời gian' })}
          error={errors.reservationTime?.message}
          defaultValue={getDefaultTime()}
        />

        <div className="form-group">
          <label className="form-label">Ghi chú:</label>
          <textarea
            {...register('note')}
            className="input"
            rows="3"
            placeholder="Ghi chú đặc biệt..."
          />
        </div>

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="flex-1">
            Xác nhận đặt bàn
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ReservationModal;

🚀 Main App Component
src/App.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';
import { POSProvider } from './context/POSContext';
import TableGrid from './components/Tables/TableGrid';
import MenuGrid from './components/Menu/MenuGrid';
import OrderPanel from './components/Order/OrderPanel';
import MenuItemModal from './components/Modals/MenuItemModal';
import PaymentModal from './components/Modals/PaymentModal';
import PrintModal from './components/Modals/PrintModal';
import './styles/globals.scss';

function App() {
  const [showMenuItemModal, setShowMenuItemModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPaymentModal, setShowPaymentModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPrintModal, setShowPrintModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedMenuItem, setSelectedMenuItem] = useState(null);

  const handleMenuItemClick = (menuItem) => {
    setSelectedMenuItem(menuItem);
    setShowMenuItemModal(true);
  };

  const handleCloseMenuItemModal = () => {
    setShowMenuItemModal(false);
    setSelectedMenuItem(null);
  };

  return (
    <POSProvider>
      <div className="pos-container">
        <div className="left-panel">
          <TableGrid />
        </div>

        <div className="center-panel">
          <MenuGrid onMenuItemClick={handleMenuItemClick} />
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <OrderPanel 
            onShowPrintModal={() => setShowPrintModal(true)}
            onProcessPayment={() => setShowPaymentModal(true)}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Modals */}
        <MenuItemModal
          isOpen={showMenuItemModal}
          onClose={handleCloseMenuItemModal}
          menuItem={selectedMenuItem}
        />

        <PaymentModal
          isOpen={showPaymentModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPaymentModal(false)}
        />

        <PrintModal
          isOpen={showPrintModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPrintModal(false)}
        />

        <Toaster 
          position="top-right"
          toastOptions={{
            duration: 4000,
            style: {
              background: '#363636',
              color: '#fff',
            },
            success: {
              style: {
                background: '#22c55e',
              },
            },
            error: {
              style: {
                background: '#ef4444',
              },
            },
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </POSProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

🔧 Utility Files
src/utils/formatters.js
export const formatPrice = (price) => {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'VND'
  }).format(price);
};

export const formatDateTime = (date) => {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleString('vi-VN');
};

export const formatTime = (date) => {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString('vi-VN');
};

export const formatDate = (date) => {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString('vi-VN');
};

📋 Cách chạy dự án
Tạo project React:
npx create-react-app foodhub-pos
cd foodhub-pos

Cài đặt dependencies:
npm install react-hook-form react-hot-toast lucide-react date-fns sass

Copy tất cả files vào đúng vị trí
Chạy dự án:
npm start

🎯 Tính năng chính đã implement:
✅ Quản lý bàn: Chọn bàn, đổi trạng thái, đặt bàn
✅ Menu: Tìm kiếm, phân loại, thêm món
✅ Đơn hàng: Thêm/xóa/sửa món, tính tổng tiền
✅ In ấn: Tự động gửi bếp/bar khi lưu đơn
✅ Responsive: Hoạt động tốt trên mobile/tablet
✅ Notifications: Toast messages cho user feedback
✅ State Management: Context API + useReducer
✅ Form Handling: React Hook Form
✅ SCSS: Modular styling với variables & mixins

Bạn có muốn tôi thêm component nào khác không? Ví dụ như PaymentModal, PrintModal, hay các tính năng khác?

tất cả các tính năng khác

Tôi sẽ cung cấp tất cả các tính năng còn lại để hoàn thiện hệ thống POS!

💳 Payment Modal
src/components/Modals/PaymentModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { CreditCard, DollarSign, Smartphone, Building2, Calculator, Receipt } from 'lucide-react';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { PAYMENT_METHODS } from '../../utils/constants';
import { formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './PaymentModal.scss';

const PaymentModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { calculateOrderSummary, clearOrder } = useOrder();
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const { register, handleSubmit, watch, setValue, reset } = useForm();
  
  const [selectedMethod, setSelectedMethod] = useState('cash');
  const [customerPaid, setCustomerPaid] = useState('');
  const [showCalculator, setShowCalculator] = useState(false);
  
  const summary = calculateOrderSummary();
  const paidAmount = parseFloat(customerPaid) || 0;
  const changeAmount = paidAmount - summary.total;

  const paymentIcons = {
    cash: DollarSign,
    card: CreditCard,
    transfer: Building2,
    ewallet: Smartphone
  };

  const handlePaymentMethodSelect = (methodId) => {
    setSelectedMethod(methodId);
    if (methodId !== 'cash') {
      setCustomerPaid(summary.total.toString());
    } else {
      setCustomerPaid('');
    }
  };

  const handleCalculatorInput = (value) => {
    if (value === 'clear') {
      setCustomerPaid('');
    } else if (value === 'exact') {
      setCustomerPaid(summary.total.toString());
    } else {
      setCustomerPaid(prev => prev + value);
    }
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (selectedMethod === 'cash' && paidAmount < summary.total) {
      toast.error('Số tiền khách đưa không đủ!');
      return;
    }

    // Process payment
    const paymentData = {
      tableCode: state.currentTable?.code,
      orderItems: state.currentOrder,
      summary,
      paymentMethod: selectedMethod,
      paidAmount,
      changeAmount: Math.max(0, changeAmount),
      timestamp: new Date(),
      cashier: 'Admin', // In real app, get from auth
      ...data
    };

    // Update table status to available
    if (state.currentTable) {
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_TABLE',
        payload: {
          tableCode: state.currentTable.code,
          updates: { 
            status: 'available',
            customerName: '',
            phone: '',
            guestCount: 0,
            note: ''
          }
        }
      });
    }

    // Clear order
    clearOrder();

    // Print receipt
    printReceipt(paymentData);

    toast.success('Thanh toán thành công!');
    handleClose();
  };

  const printReceipt = (paymentData) => {
    // Add to print queue
    const receiptItem = {
      id: Date.now(),
      type: 'receipt',
      printer: Object.values(state.printers).find(p => p.location === 'cashier'),
      data: paymentData,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      status: 'printing'
    };

    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: receiptItem });
    
    // Simulate print completion
    setTimeout(() => {
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
        payload: { id: receiptItem.id, updates: { status: 'completed' } }
      });
    }, 2000);
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    reset();
    setSelectedMethod('cash');
    setCustomerPaid('');
    setShowCalculator(false);
    onClose();
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={handleClose}
      title="Thanh toán"
      size="lg"
    >
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} className="payment-form">
        {/* Order Summary */}
        <div className="payment-summary">
          <h4>Chi tiết đơn hàng</h4>
          <div className="summary-details">
            <div className="summary-row">
              <span>Bàn:</span>
              <span>{state.currentTable?.code || 'Mang về'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="summary-row">
              <span>Số món:</span>
              <span>{state.currentOrder.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="summary-row">
              <span>Tạm tính:</span>
              <span>{summary.formatted.subtotal}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="summary-row">
              <span>Thuế VAT:</span>
              <span>{summary.formatted.tax}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="summary-row">
              <span>Phí phục vụ:</span>
              <span>{summary.formatted.service}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="summary-row total-row">
              <span>Tổng cộng:</span>
              <span>{summary.formatted.total}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Payment Methods */}
        <div className="payment-methods">
          <h4>Phương thức thanh toán</h4>
          <div className="methods-grid">
            {PAYMENT_METHODS.map(method => {
              const Icon = paymentIcons[method.id];
              return (
                <button
                  key={method.id}
                  type="button"
                  className={`method-btn ${selectedMethod === method.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => handlePaymentMethodSelect(method.id)}
                >
                  <Icon size={20} />
                  <span>{method.name}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Cash Payment Details */}
        {selectedMethod === 'cash' && (
          <div className="cash-payment">
            <div className="cash-input-section">
              <Input
                label="Khách đưa:"
                type="number"
                value={customerPaid}
                onChange={(e) => setCustomerPaid(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Nhập số tiền khách đưa"
              />
              
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="secondary"
                onClick={() => setShowCalculator(!showCalculator)}
              >
                <Calculator size={16} />
                Máy tính
              </Button>
            </div>

            {customerPaid && (
              <div className="change-calculation">
                <div className="change-row">
                  <span>Khách đưa:</span>
                  <span>{formatPrice(paidAmount)}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="change-row">
                  <span>Tổng tiền:</span>
                  <span>{summary.formatted.total}</span>
                </div>
                <div className={`change-row ${changeAmount < 0 ? 'insufficient' : 'change'}`}>
                  <span>{changeAmount < 0 ? 'Thiếu:' : 'Tiền thừa:'}</span>
                  <span>{formatPrice(Math.abs(changeAmount))}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Calculator */}
            {showCalculator && (
              <div className="calculator">
                <div className="calculator-display">
                  {customerPaid || '0'}
                </div>
                <div className="calculator-buttons">
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => handleCalculatorInput('clear')}>C</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => handleCalculatorInput('exact')}>Vừa đủ</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => handleCalculatorInput('00')}>00</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => handleCalculatorInput('000')}>000</button>
                  
                  {[7,8,9,4,5,6,1,2,3,0].map(num => (
                    <button 
                      key={num} 
                      type="button" 
                      onClick={() => handleCalculatorInput(num.toString())}
                    >
                      {num}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Customer Info */}
        <div className="customer-info">
          <h4>Thông tin khách hàng (tùy chọn)</h4>
          <div className="customer-fields">
            <Input
              label="Tên khách hàng:"
              {...register('customerName')}
              placeholder="Nhập tên khách hàng"
            />
            <Input
              label="Số điện thoại:"
              {...register('customerPhone')}
              placeholder="Nhập số điện thoại"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="payment-actions">
          <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button 
            type="submit" 
            variant="success"
            disabled={selectedMethod === 'cash' && paidAmount < summary.total}
          >
            <Receipt size={16} />
            Thanh toán & In hóa đơn
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default PaymentModal;

src/components/Modals/PaymentModal.scss
@import '../../styles/variables.scss';
@import '../../styles/mixins.scss';

.payment-form {
  .payment-summary {
    background: $gray-50;
    padding: $spacing-lg;
    border-radius: $border-radius-lg;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-lg;

    h4 {
      margin: 0 0 $spacing-md 0;
      color: $gray-900;
      font-size: $font-size-lg;
    }
  }

  .summary-details {
    .summary-row {
      @include flex-between;
      margin-bottom: $spacing-sm;
      font-size: $font-size-sm;

      &:last-child {
        margin-bottom: 0;
      }

      &.total-row {
        font-weight: 700;
        font-size: $font-size-base;
        color: $primary-color;
        border-top: 1px solid $gray-200;
        padding-top: $spacing-sm;
        margin-top: $spacing-md;
      }
    }
  }

  .payment-methods {
    margin-bottom: $spacing-lg;

    h4 {
      margin: 0 0 $spacing-md 0;
      color: $gray-900;
      font-size: $font-size-lg;
    }
  }

  .methods-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    gap: $spacing-sm;

    @include responsive(mobile) {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
  }

  .method-btn {
    padding: $spacing-lg;
    border: 2px solid $gray-200;
    background: white;
    border-radius: $border-radius-lg;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: all 0.2s;
    display: flex;
