🖨️ Print Modal
src/components/Modals/PrintModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Printer, Check, X, AlertCircle, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { useOrder } from '../../hooks/useOrder';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './PrintModal.scss';

const PrintModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const { currentOrder } = useOrder();
  const [selectedPrinters, setSelectedPrinters] = useState([]);
  const [printType, setPrintType] = useState('kitchen');

  const printers = Object.values(state.printers);
  const printQueue = state.printQueue;

  const handlePrinterToggle = (printerId) => {
    setSelectedPrinters(prev => 
      prev.includes(printerId) 
        ? prev.filter(id => id !== printerId)
        : [...prev, printerId]
    );
  };

  const handlePrintTypeChange = (type) => {
    setPrintType(type);
    // Auto-select appropriate printers
    if (type === 'kitchen') {
      const kitchenPrinters = printers.filter(p => p.location === 'kitchen' && p.status === 'online');
      setSelectedPrinters(kitchenPrinters.map(p => p.id));
    } else if (type === 'bar') {
      const barPrinters = printers.filter(p => p.location === 'bar' && p.status === 'online');
      setSelectedPrinters(barPrinters.map(p => p.id));
    } else if (type === 'bill') {
      const cashierPrinters = printers.filter(p => p.location === 'cashier' && p.status === 'online');
      setSelectedPrinters(cashierPrinters.map(p => p.id));
    }
  };

  const handlePrint = () => {
    if (selectedPrinters.length === 0) {
      toast.error('Vui lòng chọn ít nhất một máy in!');
      return;
    }

    if (currentOrder.length === 0) {
      toast.error('Không có món nào để in!');
      return;
    }

    // Filter items based on print type
    let itemsToPrint = currentOrder;
    if (printType === 'kitchen') {
      itemsToPrint = currentOrder.filter(item => {
        const menuItem = state.menuItems.find(m => m.id === item.menuItemId);
        return menuItem && ['appetizer', 'main', 'seafood', 'hotpot'].includes(menuItem.category);
      });
    } else if (printType === 'bar') {
      itemsToPrint = currentOrder.filter(item => {
        const menuItem = state.menuItems.find(m => m.id === item.menuItemId);
        return menuItem && ['drink', 'dessert'].includes(menuItem.category);
      });
    }

    if (itemsToPrint.length === 0) {
      toast.error(`Không có món ${printType === 'kitchen' ? 'bếp' : printType === 'bar' ? 'bar' : ''} nào để in!`);
      return;
    }

    // Send to selected printers
    selectedPrinters.forEach(printerId => {
      const printer = printers.find(p => p.id === printerId);
      if (printer && printer.status === 'online') {
        const queueItem = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          type: printType,
          printer,
          table: state.currentTable?.code || 'Mang về',
          order: itemsToPrint,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          status: 'printing'
        };

        dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: queueItem });
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
          payload: {
            id: printerId,
            updates: { status: 'busy', lastUsed: new Date() }
          }
        });

        // Simulate print completion
        setTimeout(() => {
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
            payload: { id: queueItem.id, updates: { status: 'completed' } }
          });
          dispatch({
            type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
            payload: {
              id: printerId,
              updates: { status: 'online' }
            }
          });
        }, 3000);
      }
    });

    toast.success(`Đã gửi ${itemsToPrint.length} món đến ${selectedPrinters.length} máy in!`);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleRetryPrint = (queueItemId) => {
    const queueItem = printQueue.find(item => item.id === queueItemId);
    if (queueItem) {
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
        payload: { id: queueItemId, updates: { status: 'printing' } }
      });

      setTimeout(() => {
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
          payload: { id: queueItemId, updates: { status: 'completed' } }
        });
      }, 2000);

      toast.success('Đã gửi lại lệnh in!');
    }
  };

  const handleClearQueue = () => {
    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_PRINT_QUEUE' });
    toast.success('Đã xóa hàng đợi in!');
  };

  const getStatusIcon = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'online': return <Check className="status-icon online" size={16} />;
      case 'offline': return <X className="status-icon offline" size={16} />;
      case 'busy': return <RefreshCw className="status-icon busy" size={16} />;
      default: return <AlertCircle className="status-icon" size={16} />;
    }
  };

  const getQueueStatusIcon = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'printing': return <RefreshCw className="queue-status printing" size={14} />;
      case 'completed': return <Check className="queue-status completed" size={14} />;
      case 'failed': return <X className="queue-status failed" size={14} />;
      default: return <AlertCircle className="queue-status" size={14} />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Quản lý in ấn"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="print-modal">
        {/* Print Type Selection */}
        <div className="print-types">
          <h4>Loại in:</h4>
          <div className="type-buttons">
            <button
              className={`type-btn ${printType === 'kitchen' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handlePrintTypeChange('kitchen')}
            >
              🍳 Bếp
            </button>
            <button
              className={`type-btn ${printType === 'bar' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handlePrintTypeChange('bar')}
            >
              🍹 Bar
            </button>
            <button
              className={`type-btn ${printType === 'bill' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => handlePrintTypeChange('bill')}
            >
              🧾 Hóa đơn
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="print-content">
          {/* Printer Selection */}
          <div className="printer-section">
            <h4>Chọn máy in:</h4>
            <div className="printers-list">
              {printers.map(printer => (
                <div
                  key={printer.id}
                  className={`printer-item ${selectedPrinters.includes(printer.id) ? 'selected' : ''} ${printer.status}`}
                  onClick={() => printer.status === 'online' && handlePrinterToggle(printer.id)}
                >
                  <div className="printer-info">
                    <div className="printer-name">
                      {getStatusIcon(printer.status)}
                      {printer.name}
                    </div>
                    <div className="printer-details">
                      <span className="printer-location">{printer.location}</span>
                      <span className="printer-ip">{printer.ip}</span>
                      <span className="printer-last-used">
                        {formatDateTime(printer.lastUsed)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {selectedPrinters.includes(printer.id) && (
                    <Check className="selected-icon" size={20} />
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Print Queue */}
          <div className="queue-section">
            <div className="queue-header">
              <h4>Hàng đợi in ({printQueue.length})</h4>
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                size="sm"
                onClick={handleClearQueue}
                disabled={printQueue.length === 0}
              >
                Xóa hàng đợi
              </Button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="queue-list">
              {printQueue.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="queue-empty">
                  Không có lệnh in nào trong hàng đợi
                </div>
              ) : (
                printQueue.map(item => (
                  <div key={item.id} className={`queue-item ${item.status}`}>
                    <div className="queue-info">
                      <div className="queue-header-info">
                        {getQueueStatusIcon(item.status)}
                        <span className="queue-type">{item.type}</span>
                        <span className="queue-table">Bàn {item.table}</span>
                        <span className="queue-time">
                          {formatDateTime(item.createdAt)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="queue-printer">
                        Máy in: {item.printer.name}
                      </div>
                      <div className="queue-items">
                        {item.order.length} món
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {item.status === 'failed' && (
                      <Button
                        variant="warning"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleRetryPrint(item.id)}
                      >
                        <RefreshCw size={14} />
                        Thử lại
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="print-actions">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Đóng
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="primary"
            onClick={handlePrint}
            disabled={selectedPrinters.length === 0 || currentOrder.length === 0}
          >
            <Printer size={16} />
            In ngay ({selectedPrinters.length} máy)
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default PrintModal;

📊 Table Info Modal
src/components/Modals/TableInfoModal.jsx
import React from 'react';
import { Clock, Users, Phone, Calendar, FileText } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import { formatDateTime } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import './TableInfoModal.scss';

const TableInfoModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, tableCode }) => {
  const { findTableByCode } = useTables();
  
  const table = findTableByCode(tableCode);

  if (!table) return null;

  const getStatusColor = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'available': return 'success';
      case 'occupied': return 'error';
      case 'reserved': return 'warning';
      default: return 'gray';
    }
  };

  const getStatusText = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'available': return 'Trống';
      case 'occupied': return 'Có khách';
      case 'reserved': return 'Đã đặt';
      default: return 'Không xác định';
    }
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Thông tin bàn ${table.code}`}
      size="md"
    >
      <div className="table-info-modal">
        <div className="table-status-section">
          <div className="status-badge">
            <span className={`status-indicator status-indicator--${getStatusColor(table.status)}`} />
            {getStatusText(table.status)}
          </div>
          <div className="capacity-info">
            <Users size={16} />
            <span>{table.capacity} chỗ ngồi</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {table.customerName && (
          <div className="customer-section">
            <h4>Thông tin khách hàng</h4>
            <div className="customer-details">
              <div className="detail-row">
                <span className="detail-label">Tên khách:</span>
                <span className="detail-value">{table.customerName}</span>
              </div>
              
              {table.phone && (
                <div className="detail-row">
                  <Phone size={14} />
                  <span className="detail-label">Điện thoại:</span>
                  <span className="detail-value">{table.phone}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              
              {table.guestCount > 0 && (
                <div className="detail-row">
                  <Users size={14} />
                  <span className="detail-label">Số khách:</span>
                  <span className="detail-value">{table.guestCount} người</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {(table.reservationTime || table.checkinTime) && (
          <div className="time-section">
            <h4>Thời gian</h4>
            <div className="time-details">
              {table.reservationTime && (
                <div className="detail-row">
                  <Calendar size={14} />
                  <span className="detail-label">Đặt bàn:</span>
                  <span className="detail-value">{formatDateTime(table.reservationTime)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              
              {table.checkinTime && (
                <div className="detail-row">
                  <Clock size={14} />
                  <span className="detail-label">Check-in:</span>
                  <span className="detail-value">{formatDateTime(table.checkinTime)}</span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {table.note && (
          <div className="note-section">
            <h4>Ghi chú</h4>
            <div className="note-content">
              <FileText size={14} />
              <span>{table.note}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose} className="w-full">
            Đóng
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default TableInfoModal;

  }

  }
}

🔄 Table Management Modals
src/components/Modals/MergeTableModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

const MergeTableModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, sourceTableCode }) => {
  const { tables, mergeTable, findTableByCode } = useTables();
  const [selectedTargetTable, setSelectedTargetTable] = useState('');

  const sourceTable = findTableByCode(sourceTableCode);
  const availableTables = Object.values(tables).flat().filter(table => 
    table.code !== sourceTableCode && 
    (table.status === 'available' || table.status === 'occupied')
  );

  const handleMerge = () => {
    if (!selectedTargetTable) {
      toast.error('Vui lòng chọn bàn đích!');
      return;
    }

    mergeTable(sourceTableCode, selectedTargetTable);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    setSelectedTargetTable('');
    onClose();
  };

  if (!sourceTable) return null;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={handleClose}
      title={`Gộp bàn ${sourceTableCode}`}
      size="md"
    >
      <div className="merge-table-modal">
        <div className="source-table-info">
          <h4>Bàn nguồn:</h4>
          <div className="table-card">
            <div className="table-code">{sourceTable.code}</div>
            <div className="table-details">
              <span>{sourceTable.capacity} chỗ</span>
              {sourceTable.customerName && <span>{sourceTable.customerName}</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="target-table-selection">
          <h4>Chọn bàn đích:</h4>
          <div className="tables-grid">
            {availableTables.map(table => (
              <div
                key={table.code}
                className={`table-option ${selectedTargetTable === table.code ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setSelectedTargetTable(table.code)}
              >
                <div className="table-code">{table.code}</div>
                <div className="table-details">
                  <span>{table.capacity} chỗ</span>
                  <span className={`status status--${table.status}`}>
                    {table.status === 'available' ? 'Trống' : 'Có khách'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                {table.customerName && (
                  <div className="customer-name">{table.customerName}</div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {selectedTargetTable && (
          <div className="merge-preview">
            <h4>Kết quả sau khi gộp:</h4>
            <div className="preview-info">
              <Link size={16} />
              <span>
                Bàn {selectedTargetTable} sẽ có {sourceTable.capacity + findTableByCode(selectedTargetTable)?.capacity} chỗ ngồi
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button 
            variant="primary" 
            onClick={handleMerge}
            disabled={!selectedTargetTable}
          >
            <Link size={16} />
            Gộp bàn
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default MergeTableModal;

src/components/Modals/MoveTableModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTables } from '../../hooks/useTables';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

const MoveTableModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, sourceTableCode }) => {
  const { tables, moveTable, findTableByCode } = useTables();
  const [selectedTargetTable, setSelectedTargetTable] = useState('');

  const sourceTable = findTableByCode(sourceTableCode);
  const availableTables = Object.values(tables).flat().filter(table => 
    table.code !== sourceTableCode && table.status === 'available'
  );

  const handleMove = () => {
    if (!selectedTargetTable) {
      toast.error('Vui lòng chọn bàn đích!');
      return;
    }

    moveTable(sourceTableCode, selectedTargetTable);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleClose = () => {
    setSelectedTargetTable('');
    onClose();
  };

  if (!sourceTable) return null;

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={handleClose}
      title={`Chuyển bàn ${sourceTableCode}`}
      size="md"
    >
      <div className="move-table-modal">
        <div className="source-table-info">
          <h4>Thông tin bàn hiện tại:</h4>
          <div className="table-card">
            <div className="table-header">
              <span className="table-code">{sourceTable.code}</span>
              <span className="table-capacity">{sourceTable.capacity} chỗ</span>
            </div>
            {sourceTable.customerName && (
              <div className="customer-info">
                <div>Khách: {sourceTable.customerName}</div>
                {sourceTable.phone && <div>SĐT: {sourceTable.phone}</div>}
                {sourceTable.guestCount > 0 && <div>Số khách: {sourceTable.guestCount}</div>}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="target-table-selection">
          <h4>Chọn bàn trống:</h4>
          {availableTables.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="no-tables">
              Không có bàn trống phù hợp
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="tables-grid">
              {availableTables.map(table => (
                <div
                  key={table.code}
                  className={`table-option ${selectedTargetTable === table.code ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedTargetTable(table.code)}
                >
                  <div className="table-code">{table.code}</div>
                  <div className="table-capacity">{table.capacity} chỗ</div>
                  <div className="status-badge available">Trống</div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button 
            variant="primary" 
            onClick={handleMove}
            disabled={!selectedTargetTable}
          >
            <RotateCcw size={16} />
            Chuyển bàn
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default MoveTableModal;

src/components/Layout/MobileMenu.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Menu, X, Home, UtensilsCrossed, Receipt, Settings } from 'lucide-react';
import './MobileMenu.scss';

const MobileMenu = ({ currentView, onViewChange }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

  const menuItems = [
    { id: 'tables', label: 'Bàn ăn', icon: Home },
    { id: 'menu', label: 'Thực đơn', icon: UtensilsCrossed },
    { id: 'orders', label: 'Đơn hàng', icon: Receipt },
    { id: 'settings', label: 'Cài đặt', icon: Settings },
  ];

  const handleItemClick = (viewId) => {
    onViewChange(viewId);
    setIsOpen(false);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <button 
        className="mobile-menu-trigger"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
      >
        {isOpen ? <X size={24} /> : <Menu size={24} />}
      </button>

      <div className={`mobile-menu-overlay ${isOpen ? 'show' : ''}`}>
        <div className="mobile-menu-content">
          <div className="mobile-menu-header">
            <h3>FoodHub POS</h3>
            <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}>
              <X size={24} />
            </button>
          </div>
          
          <nav className="mobile-menu-nav">
            {menuItems.map(item => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              return (
                <button
                  key={item.id}
                  className={`mobile-menu-item ${currentView === item.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                  onClick={() => handleItemClick(item.id)}
                >
                  <Icon size={20} />
                  <span>{item.label}</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </nav>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

export default MobileMenu;

🔧 Advanced Hooks
src/hooks/usePrinters.js
import { usePOS } from '../context/POSContext';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';

export function usePrinters() {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();

  const updatePrinter = (printerId, updates) => {
    dispatch({
      type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
      payload: { id: printerId, updates }
    });
  };

  const testPrinter = async (printerId) => {
    const printer = state.printers[printerId];
    if (!printer) return false;

    // Simulate printer test
    updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'busy' });
    
    try {
      // Simulate network request
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2000));
      
      // Random success/failure for demo
      const success = Math.random() > 0.2;
      
      if (success) {
        updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'online', lastUsed: new Date() });
        toast.success(`Máy in ${printer.name} hoạt động bình thường`);
        return true;
      } else {
        updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'offline' });
        toast.error(`Máy in ${printer.name} không phản hồi`);
        return false;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'offline' });
      toast.error(`Lỗi kết nối máy in ${printer.name}`);
      return false;
    }
  };

  const printToDevice = async (printerId, content) => {
    const printer = state.printers[printerId];
    if (!printer || printer.status !== 'online') {
      toast.error('Máy in không khả dụng');
      return false;
    }

    updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'busy' });

    try {
      // Simulate printing
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 3000));
      
      updatePrinter(printerId, { 
        status: 'online', 
        lastUsed: new Date() 
      });
      
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      updatePrinter(printerId, { status: 'offline' });
      throw error;
    }
  };

  const addToPrintQueue = (printJob) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_TO_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: printJob });
  };

  const updatePrintQueueItem = (itemId, updates) => {
    dispatch({
      type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
      payload: { id: itemId, updates }
    });
  };

  const removePrintQueueItem = (itemId) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: itemId });
  };

  const clearPrintQueue = () => {
    dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_PRINT_QUEUE' });
  };

  const getOnlinePrinters = () => {
    return Object.values(state.printers).filter(p => p.status === 'online');
  };

  const getPrintersByLocation = (location) => {
    return Object.values(state.printers).filter(p => p.location === location);
  };

  return {
    printers: state.printers,
    printQueue: state.printQueue,
    updatePrinter,
    testPrinter,
    printToDevice,
    addToPrintQueue,
    updatePrintQueueItem,
    removePrintQueueItem,
    clearPrintQueue,
    getOnlinePrinters,
    getPrintersByLocation
  };
}

src/hooks/useLocalStorage.js
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export function useLocalStorage(key, initialValue) {
  // Get from local storage then parse stored json or return initialValue
  const [storedValue, setStoredValue] = useState(() => {
    try {
      const item = window.localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : initialValue;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Error reading localStorage key "${key}":`, error);
      return initialValue;
    }
  });

  // Return a wrapped version of useState's setter function that persists the new value to localStorage
  const setValue = (value) => {
    try {
      // Allow value to be a function so we have the same API as useState
      const valueToStore = value instanceof Function ? value(storedValue) : value;
      setStoredValue(valueToStore);
      window.localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(valueToStore));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Error setting localStorage key "${key}":`, error);
    }
  };

  return [storedValue, setValue];
}

src/hooks/useKeyboard.js
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export function useKeyboard(shortcuts) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (event) => {
      const { key, ctrlKey, altKey, shiftKey } = event;
      
      // Create shortcut string
      const shortcut = [
        ctrlKey && 'ctrl',
        altKey && 'alt', 
        shiftKey && 'shift',
        key.toLowerCase()
      ].filter(Boolean).join('+');

      // Find matching shortcut
      const handler = shortcuts[shortcut];
      if (handler) {
        event.preventDefault();
        handler(event);
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [shortcuts]);
}

🎯 Settings & Configuration
src/components/Settings/SettingsModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Settings, Printer, Users, Receipt, Palette } from 'lucide-react';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import { useLocalStorage } from '../../hooks/useLocalStorage';
import './SettingsModal.scss';

const SettingsModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('general');
  const [settings, setSettings] = useLocalStorage('pos-settings', {
    restaurantName: 'FoodHub Restaurant',
    address: '123 Đường ABC, Quận 1, TP.HCM',
    phone: '028 1234 5678',
    taxRate: 10,
    serviceRate: 5,
    currency: 'VND',
    theme: 'light',
    autoSave: true,
    autoPrint: true,
    soundEnabled: true
  });

  const tabs = [
    { id: 'general', label: 'Chung', icon: Settings },
    { id: 'printers', label: 'Máy in', icon: Printer },
    { id: 'staff', label: 'Nhân viên', icon: Users },
    { id: 'receipt', label: 'Hóa đơn', icon: Receipt },
    { id: 'appearance', label: 'Giao diện', icon: Palette }
  ];

  const handleSettingChange = (key, value) => {
    setSettings(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    // Settings are automatically saved via useLocalStorage
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Cài đặt hệ thống"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="settings-modal">
        <div className="settings-sidebar">
          {tabs.map(tab => {
            const Icon = tab.icon;
            return (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                className={`settings-tab ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'active' : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
              >
                <Icon size={16} />
                <span>{tab.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="settings-content">
          {activeTab === 'general' && (
            <div className="settings-section">
              <h3>Thông tin nhà hàng</h3>
              
              <Input
                label="Tên nhà hàng:"
                value={settings.restaurantName}
                onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('restaurantName', e.target.value)}
              />
              
              <Input
                label="Địa chỉ:"
                value={settings.address}
                onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('address', e.target.value)}
              />
              
              <Input
                label="Số điện thoại:"
                value={settings.phone}
                onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('phone', e.target.value)}
              />

              <div className="settings-row">
                <Input
                  label="Thuế VAT (%):"
                  type="number"
                  value={settings.taxRate}
                  onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('taxRate', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                />
                
                <Input
                  label="Phí phục vụ (%):"
                  type="number"
                  value={settings.serviceRate}
                  onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('serviceRate', parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="checkbox-group">
                <label className="checkbox-label">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={settings.autoSave}
                    onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('autoSave', e.target.checked)}
                  />
                  <span>Tự động lưu đơn hàng</span>
                </label>
                
                <label className="checkbox-label">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={settings.autoPrint}
                    onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('autoPrint', e.target.checked)}
                  />
                  <span>Tự động in khi lưu đơn</span>
                </label>
                
                <label className="checkbox-label">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={settings.soundEnabled}
                    onChange={(e) => handleSettingChange('soundEnabled', e.target.checked)}
                  />
                  <span>Bật âm thanh thông báo</span>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'appearance' && (
            <div className="settings-section">
              <h3>Giao diện</h3>
              
              <div className="theme-selection">
                <label className="form-label">Chủ đề:</label>
                <div className="theme-options">
                  <button
                    className={`theme-option ${settings.theme === 'light' ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => handleSettingChange('theme', 'light')}
                  >
                    <div className="theme-preview light-theme"></div>
                    <span>Sáng</span>
                  </button>
                  
                  <button
                    className={`theme-option ${settings.theme === 'dark' ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                    onClick={() => handleSettingChange('theme', 'dark')}
                  >
                    <div className="theme-preview dark-theme"></div>
                    <span>Tối</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Add other tab contents here */}
        </div>

        <div className="settings-actions">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Hủy
          </Button>
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleSave}>
            Lưu cài đặt
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default SettingsModal;

import React, { useState } from 'react';

import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';

const ReportsModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [dateRange, setDateRange] = useState({
    from: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    to: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
  });

  // Mock data - in real app, fetch from API
  const mockData = {

    ],
    hourlyRevenue: [
      { hour: '11:00', revenue: 850000 },
      { hour: '12:00', revenue: 1200000 },
      { hour: '13:00', revenue: 980000 },
      { hour: '18:00', revenue: 1500000 },
      { hour: '19:00', revenue: 2100000 },
      { hour: '20:00', revenue: 1800000 }
    ]
  };

  const handleExport = (format) => {
    // Implement export functionality
    console.log(`Exporting report as ${format}`);
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Báo cáo bán hàng"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="reports-modal">
        <div className="reports-header">
          <div className="date-range-selector">
            <Input
              label="Từ ngày:"
              type="date"
              value={dateRange.from}
              onChange={(e) => setDateRange(prev => ({ ...prev, from: e.target.value }))}
            />
            <Input
              label="Đến ngày:"
              type="date"
              value={dateRange.to}
              onChange={(e) => setDateRange(prev => ({ ...prev, to: e.target.value }))}
            />
            <Button variant="primary">
              Xem báo cáo
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="reports-content">
          {/* Summary Cards */}
          <div className="summary-cards">
            <div className="summary-card revenue">
              <div className="card-icon">
                <DollarSign size={24} />
              </div>
              <div className="card-content">
                <div className="card-value">{formatPrice(mockData.revenue)}</div>
                <div className="card-label">Doanh thu</div>
                <div className="card-change positive">+12.5%</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="summary-card orders">
              <div className="card-icon">
                <BarChart3 size={24} />
              </div>
              <div className="card-content">
                <div className="card-value">{mockData.orders}</div>
                <div className="card-label">Đơn hàng</div>
                <div className="card-change positive">+8.3%</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="summary-card customers">
              <div className="card-icon">
                <Users size={24} />
              </div>
              <div className="card-content">
                <div className="card-value">{mockData.customers}</div>
                <div className="card-label">Khách hàng</div>
                <div className="card-change positive">+15.2%</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="summary-card avg-order">
              <div className="card-icon">
                <TrendingUp size={24} />
              </div>
              <div className="card-content">
                <div className="card-value">{formatPrice(mockData.avgOrderValue)}</div>
                <div className="card-label">Đơn hàng TB</div>
                <div className="card-change positive">+5.7%</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Charts and Tables */}
          <div className="reports-grid">
            <div className="report-section">
              <h4>Món bán chạy</h4>
              <div className="top-items-list">
                {mockData.topItems.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={index} className="top-item">
                    <div className="item-rank">#{index + 1}</div>
                    <div className="item-info">
                      <div className="item-name">{item.name}</div>
                      <div className="item-stats">
                        {item.quantity} phần • {formatPrice(item.revenue)}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="report-section">
              <h4>Doanh thu theo giờ</h4>
              <div className="hourly-chart">
                {mockData.hourlyRevenue.map((data, index) => (
                  <div key={index} className="chart-bar">
                    <div 
                      className="bar"
                      style={{ 
                        height: `${(data.revenue / 2100000) * 100}%` 
                      }}
                    />
                    <div className="bar-label">{data.hour}</div>
                    <div className="bar-value">{formatPrice(data.revenue)}</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="reports-actions">
          <div className="export-buttons">
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => handleExport('pdf')}>
              Xuất PDF
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => handleExport('excel')}>
              Xuất Excel
            </Button>
          </div>
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
            Đóng
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default ReportsModal;

🔄 Updated Main App with All Features
src/App.jsx (Updated)
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';
import { POSProvider } from './context/POSContext';
import { useKeyboard } from './hooks/useKeyboard';
import TableGrid from './components/Tables/TableGrid';
import MenuGrid from './components/Menu/MenuGrid';
import OrderPanel from './components/Order/OrderPanel';
import MobileMenu from './components/Layout/MobileMenu';

// Modals
import MenuItemModal from './components/Modals/MenuItemModal';
import PaymentModal from './components/Modals/PaymentModal';
import PrintModal from './components/Modals/PrintModal';
import ReservationModal from './components/Modals/ReservationModal';
import TableInfoModal from './components/Modals/TableInfoModal';
import MergeTableModal from './components/Modals/MergeTableModal';
import MoveTableModal from './components/Modals/MoveTableModal';
import SettingsModal from './components/Settings/SettingsModal';
import ReportsModal from './components/Reports/ReportsModal';

import './styles/globals.scss';

function App() {
  // Modal states
  const [showMenuItemModal, setShowMenuItemModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPaymentModal, setShowPaymentModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPrintModal, setShowPrintModal] = useState(false);
  const [showReservationModal, setShowReservationModal] = useState(false);
  const [showTableInfoModal, setShowTableInfoModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMergeTableModal, setShowMergeTableModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMoveTableModal, setShowMoveTableModal] = useState(false);
  const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showReportsModal, setShowReportsModal] = useState(false);

  // Data states
  const [selectedMenuItem, setSelectedMenuItem] = useState(null);
  const [selectedTableCode, setSelectedTableCode] = useState(null);
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('tables');

  // Keyboard shortcuts
  useKeyboard({
    'ctrl+s': () => console.log('Save shortcut'),
    'ctrl+p': () => setShowPrintModal(true),
    'ctrl+n': () => setShowReservationModal(true),
    'f1': () => setShowSettingsModal(true),
    'f2': () => setShowReportsModal(true),
    'escape': () => {
      // Close any open modal
      setShowMenuItemModal(false);
      setShowPaymentModal(false);
      setShowPrintModal(false);
      setShowReservationModal(false);
      setShowTableInfoModal(false);
      setShowMergeTableModal(false);
      setShowMoveTableModal(false);
      setShowSettingsModal(false);
      setShowReportsModal(false);
    }
  });

  const handleMenuItemClick = (menuItem) => {
    setSelectedMenuItem(menuItem);
    setShowMenuItemModal(true);
  };

  const handleCloseMenuItemModal = () => {
    setShowMenuItemModal(false);
    setSelectedMenuItem(null);
  };

  const handleTableInfo = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowTableInfoModal(true);
  };

  const handleMergeTable = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowMergeTableModal(true);
  };

  const handleMoveTable = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowMoveTableModal(true);
  };

  return (
    <POSProvider>
      <div className="pos-container">
        <MobileMenu 
          currentView={currentView} 
          onViewChange={setCurrentView} 
        />

        <div className="left-panel">
          <TableGrid 
            onTableInfo={handleTableInfo}
            onMergeTable={handleMergeTable}
            onMoveTable={handleMoveTable}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="center-panel">
          <MenuGrid onMenuItemClick={handleMenuItemClick} />
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <OrderPanel 
            onShowPrintModal={() => setShowPrintModal(true)}
            onProcessPayment={() => setShowPaymentModal(true)}
          />
        </div>

        {/* All Modals */}
        <MenuItemModal
          isOpen={showMenuItemModal}
          onClose={handleCloseMenuItemModal}
          menuItem={selectedMenuItem}
        />

        <PaymentModal
          isOpen={showPaymentModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPaymentModal(false)}
        />

        <PrintModal
          isOpen={showPrintModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPrintModal(false)}
        />

        <ReservationModal
          isOpen={showReservationModal}
          onClose={() => setShowReservationModal(false)}
        />

        <TableInfoModal
          isOpen={showTableInfoModal}
          onClose={() => setShowTableInfoModal(false)}
          tableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <MergeTableModal
          isOpen={showMergeTableModal}
          onClose={() => setShowMergeTableModal(false)}
          sourceTableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <MoveTableModal
          isOpen={showMoveTableModal}
          onClose={() => setShowMoveTableModal(false)}
          sourceTableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <SettingsModal
          isOpen={showSettingsModal}
          onClose={() => setShowSettingsModal(false)}
        />

        <ReportsModal
          isOpen={showReportsModal}
          onClose={() => setShowReportsModal(false)}
        />

        <Toaster 
          position="top-right"
          toastOptions={{
            duration: 4000,
            style: {
              background: '#363636',
              color: '#fff',
            },
            success: {
              style: {
                background: '#22c55e',
              },
            },
            error: {
              style: {
                background: '#ef4444',
              },
            },
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </POSProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

🖨️ Print Queue Modal
src/components/Modals/PrintQueueModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Printer, RefreshCw, Check, X, AlertCircle, Trash2, Play, Pause } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { formatDateTime, formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './PrintQueueModal.scss';

const PrintQueueModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const [selectedItems, setSelectedItems] = useState([]);

  const handleSelectItem = (itemId) => {
    setSelectedItems(prev => 
      prev.includes(itemId) 
        ? prev.filter(id => id !== itemId)
        : [...prev, itemId]
    );
  };

  const handleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedItems.length === state.printQueue.length) {
      setSelectedItems([]);
    } else {
      setSelectedItems(state.printQueue.map(item => item.id));
    }
  };

  const handleRetrySelected = () => {
    selectedItems.forEach(itemId => {
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
        payload: { id: itemId, updates: { status: 'printing' } }
      });

      // Simulate retry
      setTimeout(() => {
        const success = Math.random() > 0.2;
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
          payload: { 
            id: itemId, 
            updates: { status: success ? 'completed' : 'failed' } 
          }
        });
      }, 2000);
    });

    toast.success(`Đã thử lại ${selectedItems.length} lệnh in`);
    setSelectedItems([]);
  };

  const handleDeleteSelected = () => {
    selectedItems.forEach(itemId => {
      dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: itemId });
    });
    
    toast.success(`Đã xóa ${selectedItems.length} lệnh in`);
    setSelectedItems([]);
  };

  const handleClearCompleted = () => {
    const completedItems = state.printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'completed');
    completedItems.forEach(item => {
      dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: item.id });
    });
    
    toast.success(`Đã xóa ${completedItems.length} lệnh in hoàn thành`);
  };

  const handlePauseQueue = () => {
    // Implement queue pause logic
    toast.info('Đã tạm dừng hàng đợi in');
  };

  const handleResumeQueue = () => {
    // Implement queue resume logic
    toast.info('Đã tiếp tục hàng đợi in');
  };

  const getStatusIcon = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'printing':
        return <RefreshCw className="status-icon printing" size={16} />;
      case 'completed':
        return <Check className="status-icon completed" size={16} />;
      case 'failed':
        return <X className="status-icon failed" size={16} />;
      case 'pending':
        return <Pause className="status-icon pending" size={16} />;
      default:
        return <AlertCircle className="status-icon" size={16} />;
    }
  };

  const getStatusText = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'printing': return 'Đang in';
      case 'completed': return 'Hoàn thành';
      case 'failed': return 'Thất bại';
      case 'pending': return 'Chờ xử lý';
      default: return 'Không xác định';
    }
  };

  const getTypeIcon = (type) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'kitchen': return '🍳';
      case 'bar': return '🍹';
      case 'receipt': return '🧾';
      case 'bill': return '💰';
      default: return '📄';
    }
  };

  const groupedQueue = state.printQueue.reduce((groups, item) => {
    const key = item.status;
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = [];
    groups[key].push(item);
    return groups;
  }, {});

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Hàng đợi in"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="print-queue-modal">
        {/* Queue Controls */}
        <div className="queue-controls">
          <div className="queue-stats">
            <div className="stat-item">
              <span className="stat-label">Tổng:</span>
              <span className="stat-value">{state.printQueue.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="stat-item">
              <span className="stat-label">Đang in:</span>
              <span className="stat-value printing">
                {state.printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'printing').length}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="stat-item">
              <span className="stat-label">Hoàn thành:</span>
              <span className="stat-value completed">
                {state.printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'completed').length}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="stat-item">
              <span className="stat-label">Thất bại:</span>
              <span className="stat-value failed">
                {state.printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'failed').length}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="queue-actions">
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleSelectAll}
            >
              {selectedItems.length === state.printQueue.length ? 'Bỏ chọn tất cả' : 'Chọn tất cả'}
            </Button>
            
            <Button
              variant="warning"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleRetrySelected}
              disabled={selectedItems.length === 0}
            >
              <RefreshCw size={14} />
              Thử lại ({selectedItems.length})
            </Button>
            
            <Button
              variant="error"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleDeleteSelected}
              disabled={selectedItems.length === 0}
            >
              <Trash2 size={14} />
              Xóa ({selectedItems.length})
            </Button>
            
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleClearCompleted}
            >
              Xóa hoàn thành
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Queue List */}
        <div className="queue-content">
          {state.printQueue.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="queue-empty">
              <Printer size={48} />
              <h3>Hàng đợi trống</h3>
              <p>Chưa có lệnh in nào trong hàng đợi</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="queue-list">
              {Object.entries(groupedQueue).map(([status, items]) => (
                <div key={status} className="queue-group">
                  <div className="group-header">
                    {getStatusIcon(status)}
                    <span className="group-title">
                      {getStatusText(status)} ({items.length})
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="group-items">
                    {items.map(item => (
                      <div 
                        key={item.id} 
                        className={`queue-item ${item.status} ${selectedItems.includes(item.id) ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                      >
                        <div className="item-checkbox">
                          <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={selectedItems.includes(item.id)}
                            onChange={() => handleSelectItem(item.id)}
                          />
                        </div>

                        <div className="item-info">
                          <div className="item-header">
                            <div className="item-type">
                              <span className="type-icon">{getTypeIcon(item.type)}</span>
                              <span className="type-text">{item.type}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="item-table">Bàn {item.table}</div>
                            <div className="item-time">
                              {formatDateTime(item.createdAt)}
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="item-printer">
                            <Printer size={14} />
                            <span>{item.printer.name}</span>
                            <span className="printer-ip">({item.printer.ip})</span>
                          </div>

                          <div className="item-details">
                            <div className="order-summary">
                              {item.order.slice(0, 3).map((orderItem, index) => (
                                <div key={index} className="order-item-summary">
                                  <span className="item-name">{orderItem.name}</span>
                                  <span className="item-qty">x{orderItem.quantity}</span>
                                </div>
                              ))}
                              {item.order.length > 3 && (
                                <div className="more-items">
                                  +{item.order.length - 3} món khác
                                </div>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            
                            <div className="order-total">
                              Tổng: {formatPrice(item.order.reduce((sum, orderItem) => sum + orderItem.total, 0))}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="item-actions">
                          {item.status === 'failed' && (
                            <Button
                              variant="warning"
                              size="sm"
                              onClick={() => handleRetrySelected([item.id])}
                            >
                              <RefreshCw size={12} />
                            </Button>
                          )}
                          
                          <Button
                            variant="error"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteSelected([item.id])}
                          >
                            <Trash2 size={12} />
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Modal Actions */}
        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
            Đóng
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default PrintQueueModal;

src/components/Modals/PrintQueueModal.scss
@import '../../styles/variables.scss';
@import '../../styles/mixins.scss';

.print-queue-modal {
  .queue-controls {
    @include flex-between;
    padding: $spacing-lg;
    background: $gray-50;
    border-radius: $border-radius-lg;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-lg;

    @include responsive(mobile) {
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: $spacing-md;
    }
  }

  .queue-stats {
    display: flex;
    gap: $spacing-lg;

    @include responsive(mobile) {
      justify-content: space-between;
      width: 100%;
    }
  }

  .stat-item {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

👥 Staff Management Modal
src/components/Modals/StaffModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Users, Plus, Edit, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import { useLocalStorage } from '../../hooks/useLocalStorage';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './StaffModal.scss';

const StaffModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useLocalStorage('pos-staff', [
    {
      id: 1,
      name: 'Nguyễn Văn Admin',
      username: 'admin',
      role: 'admin',
      phone: '0901234567',
      email: 'admin@foodhub.com',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
      lastLogin: new Date()
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      name: 'Trần Thị Thu Ngân',
      username: 'cashier1',
      role: 'cashier',
      phone: '0987654321',
      email: 'cashier1@foodhub.com',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-05'),
      lastLogin: new Date(Date.now() - 3600000)
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      name: 'Lê Văn Phục Vụ',
      username: 'waiter1',
      role: 'waiter',
      phone: '0912345678',
      email: 'waiter1@foodhub.com',
      status: 'inactive',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-10'),
      lastLogin: new Date(Date.now() - 86400000)
    }
  ]);

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingStaff, setEditingStaff] = useState(null);
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm();

  const roles = [
    { value: 'admin', label: 'Quản trị viên', color: '#ef4444' },
    { value: 'manager', label: 'Quản lý', color: '#f59e0b' },
    { value: 'cashier', label: 'Thu ngân', color: '#3b82f6' },
    { value: 'waiter', label: 'Phục vụ', color: '#22c55e' },
    { value: 'kitchen', label: 'Bếp', color: '#8b5cf6' }
  ];

  const handleAddStaff = () => {
    setEditingStaff(null);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset();
  };

  const handleEditStaff = (staffMember) => {
    setEditingStaff(staffMember);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset({
      name: staffMember.name,
      username: staffMember.username,
      role: staffMember.role,
      phone: staffMember.phone,
      email: staffMember.email,
      status: staffMember.status
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteStaff = (staffId) => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa nhân viên này?')) {
      setStaff(prev => prev.filter(s => s.id !== staffId));
      toast.success('Đã xóa nhân viên');
    }
  };

  const handleToggleStatus = (staffId) => {
    setStaff(prev => prev.map(s => 
      s.id === staffId 
        ? { ...s, status: s.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active' }
        : s
    ));
    toast.success('Đã cập nhật trạng thái nhân viên');
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (editingStaff) {
      // Update existing staff
      setStaff(prev => prev.map(s => 
        s.id === editingStaff.id 
          ? { ...s, ...data }
          : s
      ));
      toast.success('Đã cập nhật thông tin nhân viên');
    } else {
      // Add new staff
      const newStaff = {
        id: Date.now(),
        ...data,
        createdAt: new Date(),
        lastLogin: null
      };
      setStaff(prev => [...prev, newStaff]);
      toast.success('Đã thêm nhân viên mới');
    }
    
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingStaff(null);
    reset();
  };

  const handleCloseForm = () => {
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingStaff(null);
    reset();
  };

  const getRoleInfo = (role) => {
    return roles.find(r => r.value === role) || roles[0];
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status) => {
    return status === 'active' ? '#22c55e' : '#6b7280';
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Quản lý nhân viên"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="staff-modal">
        {!showForm ? (
          <>
            {/* Staff List Header */}
            <div className="staff-header">
              <div className="staff-stats">
                <div className="stat-card">
                  <div className="stat-number">{staff.length}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Tổng nhân viên</div>
                </div>
                <div className="stat-card">
                  <div className="stat-number">{staff.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Đang hoạt động</div>
                </div>
                <div className="stat-card">
                  <div className="stat-number">{staff.filter(s => s.status === 'inactive').length}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Tạm nghỉ</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleAddStaff}>
                <Plus size={16} />
                Thêm nhân viên
              </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Staff List */}
            <div className="staff-list">
              {staff.map(staffMember => {
                const roleInfo = getRoleInfo(staffMember.role);
                return (
                  <div key={staffMember.id} className="staff-item">
                    <div className="staff-avatar">
                      <Users size={24} />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="staff-info">
                      <div className="staff-name">{staffMember.name}</div>
                      <div className="staff-details">
                        <span className="staff-username">@{staffMember.username}</span>
                        <span 
                          className="staff-role"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: roleInfo.color }}
                        >
                          {roleInfo.label}
                        </span>
                        <span 
                          className="staff-status"
                          style={{ color: getStatusColor(staffMember.status) }}
                        >
                          {staffMember.status === 'active' ? 'Hoạt động' : 'Tạm nghỉ'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="staff-contact">
                        <span>{staffMember.phone}</span>
                        <span>{staffMember.email}</span>
                      </div>
                      {staffMember.lastLogin && (
                        <div className="staff-last-login">
                          Đăng nhập cuối: {staffMember.lastLogin.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="staff-actions">
                      <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleEditStaff(staffMember)}
                      >
                        <Edit size={14} />
                      </Button>
                      
                      <Button
                        variant={staffMember.status === 'active' ? 'warning' : 'success'}
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleStatus(staffMember.id)}
                      >
                        {staffMember.status === 'active' ? 'Tạm nghỉ' : 'Kích hoạt'}
                      </Button>
                      
                      <Button
                        variant="error"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteStaff(staffMember.id)}
                        disabled={staffMember.role === 'admin'}
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* Staff Form */
          <div className="staff-form">
            <div className="form-header">
              <h3>{editingStaff ? 'Sửa thông tin nhân viên' : 'Thêm nhân viên mới'}</h3>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                <X size={16} />
              </Button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
              <div className="form-grid">
                <Input
                  label="Họ và tên:"
                  {...register('name', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập họ tên' })}
                  error={errors.name?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập họ và tên"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tên đăng nhập:"
                  {...register('username', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tên đăng nhập' })}
                  error={errors.username?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập tên đăng nhập"
                />

                <div className="form-group">
                  <label className="form-label">Vai trò:</label>
                  <select
                    {...register('role', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn vai trò' })}
                    className="input"
                  >
                    <option value="">Chọn vai trò</option>
                    {roles.map(role => (
                      <option key={role.value} value={role.value}>
                        {role.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  {errors.role && (
                    <span className="input-error">{errors.role.message}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Input
                  label="Số điện thoại:"
                  type="tel"
                  {...register('phone', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại' })}
                  error={errors.phone?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập số điện thoại"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Email:"
                  type="email"
                  {...register('email', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập email' })}
                  error={errors.email?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập địa chỉ email"
                />

                {!editingStaff && (
                  <div className="form-group">
                    <label className="form-label">Mật khẩu:</label>
                    <div className="password-input">
                      <input
                        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        {...register('password', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu' })}
                        className="input"
                        placeholder="Nhập mật khẩu"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="password-toggle"
                        onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                      >
                        {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    {errors.password && (
                      <span className="input-error">{errors.password.message}</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="form-group">
                  <label className="form-label">Trạng thái:</label>
                  <select
                    {...register('status', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn trạng thái' })}
                    className="input"
                  >
                    <option value="active">Hoạt động</option>
                    <option value="inactive">Tạm nghỉ</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="form-actions">
                <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
                  {editingStaff ? 'Cập nhật' : 'Thêm mới'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Modal Actions */}
        {!showForm && (
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Đóng
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default StaffModal;
