🏪 Inventory Modal
src/components/Modals/InventoryModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Package, Plus, Edit, AlertTriangle, TrendingDown, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import { useLocalStorage } from '../../hooks/useLocalStorage';
import { formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './InventoryModal.scss';

const InventoryModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useLocalStorage('pos-inventory', [
    {
      id: 1,
      name: 'Thịt bò thăn',
      category: 'meat',
      unit: 'kg',
      currentStock: 15,
      minStock: 5,
      maxStock: 50,
      costPrice: 450000,
      supplier: 'Công ty Thịt Sạch ABC',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'in_stock'
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      name: 'Tôm hùm',
      category: 'seafood',
      unit: 'con',
      currentStock: 3,
      minStock: 5,
      maxStock: 20,
      costPrice: 350000,
      supplier: 'Hải sản Tươi Sống',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'low_stock'
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      name: 'Rau xà lách',
      category: 'vegetable',
      unit: 'kg',
      currentStock: 0,
      minStock: 2,
      maxStock: 10,
      costPrice: 25000,
      supplier: 'Nông trại Xanh',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'out_of_stock'
    }
  ]);

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState('all');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('all');

  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm();

  const categories = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'Tất cả' },
    { value: 'meat', label: 'Thịt' },
    { value: 'seafood', label: 'Hải sản' },
    { value: 'vegetable', label: 'Rau củ' },
    { value: 'spice', label: 'Gia vị' },
    { value: 'beverage', label: 'Đồ uống' },
    { value: 'other', label: 'Khác' }
  ];

  const statuses = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'Tất cả' },
    { value: 'in_stock', label: 'Còn hàng' },
    { value: 'low_stock', label: 'Sắp hết' },
    { value: 'out_of_stock', label: 'Hết hàng' }
  ];

  const getStatusInfo = (item) => {
    if (item.currentStock === 0) {
      return { status: 'out_of_stock', label: 'Hết hàng', color: '#ef4444' };
    } else if (item.currentStock <= item.minStock) {
      return { status: 'low_stock', label: 'Sắp hết', color: '#f59e0b' };
    } else {
      return { status: 'in_stock', label: 'Còn hàng', color: '#22c55e' };
    }
  };

  const filteredInventory = inventory.filter(item => {
    const matchesSearch = !searchTerm || 
      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.supplier.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesCategory = filterCategory === 'all' || item.category === filterCategory;
    
    const statusInfo = getStatusInfo(item);
    const matchesStatus = filterStatus === 'all' || statusInfo.status === filterStatus;
    
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory && matchesStatus;
  });

  const handleAddItem = () => {
    setEditingItem(null);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset();
  };

  const handleEditItem = (item) => {
    setEditingItem(item);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset({
      name: item.name,
      category: item.category,
      unit: item.unit,
      currentStock: item.currentStock,
      minStock: item.minStock,
      maxStock: item.maxStock,
      costPrice: item.costPrice,
      supplier: item.supplier
    });
  };

  const handleUpdateStock = (itemId, newStock) => {
    setInventory(prev => prev.map(item => 
      item.id === itemId 
        ? { ...item, currentStock: newStock, lastUpdated: new Date() }
        : item
    ));
    toast.success('Đã cập nhật tồn kho');
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (editingItem) {
      setInventory(prev => prev.map(item => 
        item.id === editingItem.id 
          ? { ...item, ...data, lastUpdated: new Date() }
          : item
      ));
      toast.success('Đã cập nhật thông tin nguyên liệu');
    } else {
      const newItem = {
        id: Date.now(),
        ...data,
        lastUpdated: new Date()
      };
      setInventory(prev => [...prev, newItem]);
      toast.success('Đã thêm nguyên liệu mới');
    }
    
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingItem(null);
    reset();
  };

  const handleCloseForm = () => {
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingItem(null);
    reset();
  };

  const lowStockItems = inventory.filter(item => {
    const statusInfo = getStatusInfo(item);
    return statusInfo.status === 'low_stock' || statusInfo.status === 'out_of_stock';
  });

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Quản lý kho"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="inventory-modal">
        {!showForm ? (
          <>
            {/* Inventory Header */}
            <div className="inventory-header">
              <div className="inventory-stats">
                <div className="stat-card total">
                  <Package size={20} />
                  <div className="stat-info">
                    <div className="stat-number">{inventory.length}</div>
                    <div className="stat-label">Tổng mặt hàng</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="stat-card in-stock">
                  <div className="stat-info">
                    <div className="stat-number">
                      {inventory.filter(item => getStatusInfo(item).status === 'in_stock').length}
                    </div>
                    <div className="stat-label">Còn hàng</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="stat-card low-stock">
                  <AlertTriangle size={20} />
                  <div className="stat-info">
                    <div className="stat-number">{lowStockItems.length}</div>
                    <div className="stat-label">Cần nhập thêm</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleAddItem}>
                <Plus size={16} />
                Thêm nguyên liệu
              </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Filters */}
            <div className="inventory-filters">
              <div className="search-box">
                <Search size={16} />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Tìm kiếm nguyên liệu..."
                  value={searchTerm}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                  className="search-input"
                />
              </div>
              
              <select
                value={filterCategory}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterCategory(e.target.value)}
                className="filter-select"
              >
                {categories.map(cat => (
                  <option key={cat.value} value={cat.value}>{cat.label}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
              
              <select
                value={filterStatus}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}
                className="filter-select"
              >
                {statuses.map(status => (
                  <option key={status.value} value={status.value}>{status.label}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            {/* Low Stock Alert */}
            {lowStockItems.length > 0 && (
              <div className="low-stock-alert">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} />
                <span>
                  Có {lowStockItems.length} mặt hàng cần nhập thêm: {' '}
                  {lowStockItems.slice(0, 3).map(item => item.name).join(', ')}
                  {lowStockItems.length > 3 && ` và ${lowStockItems.length - 3} mặt hàng khác`}
                </span>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Inventory List */}
            <div className="inventory-list">
              {filteredInventory.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="inventory-empty">
                  <Package size={48} />
                  <h3>Không tìm thấy nguyên liệu</h3>
                  <p>Thử thay đổi bộ lọc hoặc thêm nguyên liệu mới</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                filteredInventory.map(item => {
                  const statusInfo = getStatusInfo(item);
                  return (
                    <div key={item.id} className={`inventory-item ${statusInfo.status}`}>
                      <div className="item-info">
                        <div className="item-header">
                          <div className="item-name">{item.name}</div>
                          <div 
                            className="item-status"
                            style={{ color: statusInfo.color }}
                          >
                            {statusInfo.label}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="item-details">
                          <span className="item-category">
                            {categories.find(c => c.value === item.category)?.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="item-supplier">{item.supplier}</span>
                          <span className="item-cost">{formatPrice(item.costPrice)}/{item.unit}</span>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="stock-info">
                          <div className="stock-current">
                            Tồn kho: <strong>{item.currentStock} {item.unit}</strong>
                          </div>
                          <div className="stock-range">
                            Min: {item.minStock} | Max: {item.maxStock}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="item-actions">
                        <div className="stock-controls">
                          <Button
                            variant="secondary"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleUpdateStock(item.id, Math.max(0, item.currentStock - 1))}
                          >
                            -1
                          </Button>
                          <span className="stock-display">{item.currentStock}</span>
                          <Button
                            variant="secondary"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleUpdateStock(item.id, item.currentStock + 1)}
                          >
                            +1
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                        
                        <Button
                          variant="primary"
                          size="sm"
                          onClick={() => handleEditItem(item)}
                        >
                          <Edit size={14} />
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* Inventory Form */
          <div className="inventory-form">
            <div className="form-header">
              <h3>{editingItem ? 'Sửa nguyên liệu' : 'Thêm nguyên liệu mới'}</h3>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                ×
              </Button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
              <div className="form-grid">
                <Input
                  label="Tên nguyên liệu:"
                  {...register('name', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tên nguyên liệu' })}
                  error={errors.name?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập tên nguyên liệu"
                />

                <div className="form-group">
                  <label className="form-label">Danh mục:</label>
                  <select
                    {...register('category', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn danh mục' })}
                    className="input"
                  >
                    <option value="">Chọn danh mục</option>
                    {categories.slice(1).map(cat => (
                      <option key={cat.value} value={cat.value}>{cat.label}</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  {errors.category && (
                    <span className="input-error">{errors.category.message}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Input
                  label="Đơn vị tính:"
                  {...register('unit', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập đơn vị tính' })}
                  error={errors.unit?.message}
                  placeholder="kg, lít, gói..."
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tồn kho hiện tại:"
                  type="number"
                  {...register('currentStock', { 
                    required: 'Vui lòng nhập số lượng tồn kho',
                    min: { value: 0, message: 'Số lượng không được âm' }
                  })}
                  error={errors.currentStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tồn kho tối thiểu:"
                  type="number"
                  {...register('minStock', { 
                    required: 'Vui lòng nhập tồn kho tối thiểu',
                    min: { value: 0, message: 'Số lượng không được âm' }
                  })}
                  error={errors.minStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tồn kho tối đa:"
                  type="number"
                  {...register('maxStock', { 
                    required: 'Vui lòng nhập tồn kho tối đa',
                    min: { value: 1, message: 'Số lượng phải lớn hơn 0' }
                  })}
                  error={errors.maxStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Giá nhập:"
                  type="number"
                  {...register('costPrice', { 
                    required: 'Vui lòng nhập giá nhập',
                    min: { value: 0, message: 'Giá không được âm' }
                  })}
                  error={errors.costPrice?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Nhà cung cấp:"
                  {...register('supplier', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập nhà cung cấp' })}
                  error={errors.supplier?.message}
                  placeholder="Tên nhà cung cấp"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="form-actions">
                <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
                  {editingItem ? 'Cập nhật' : 'Thêm mới'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Modal Actions */}
        {!showForm && (
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Đóng
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default InventoryModal;

🔄 Updated App.jsx với tất cả Modal
Bạn đúng rồi! Tôi còn thiếu một số modal quan trọng. Để tôi cung cấp đầy đủ tất cả các modal còn lại:

🖨️ Print Queue Modal
src/components/Modals/PrintQueueModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Printer, Check, X, RefreshCw, Trash2, AlertCircle, Clock } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { formatDateTime, formatTime } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './PrintQueueModal.scss';

const PrintQueueModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');

  const printQueue = state.printQueue;
  const printers = Object.values(state.printers);

  const filteredQueue = printQueue.filter(item => {
    if (filter === 'all') return true;
    return item.status === filter;
  });

  const getStatusIcon = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'printing':
        return <RefreshCw className="status-icon printing" size={16} />;
      case 'completed':
        return <Check className="status-icon completed" size={16} />;
      case 'failed':
        return <X className="status-icon failed" size={16} />;
      case 'pending':
        return <Clock className="status-icon pending" size={16} />;
      default:
        return <AlertCircle className="status-icon" size={16} />;
    }
  };

  const getStatusText = (status) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'printing': return 'Đang in';
      case 'completed': return 'Hoàn thành';
      case 'failed': return 'Thất bại';
      case 'pending': return 'Chờ xử lý';
      default: return 'Không xác định';
    }
  };

  const handleRetryPrint = (queueItemId) => {
    const queueItem = printQueue.find(item => item.id === queueItemId);
    if (queueItem) {
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
        payload: { id: queueItemId, updates: { status: 'printing' } }
      });

      // Update printer status
      dispatch({
        type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
        payload: {
          id: queueItem.printer.id,
          updates: { status: 'busy', lastUsed: new Date() }
        }
      });

      // Simulate print completion
      setTimeout(() => {
        const success = Math.random() > 0.2; // 80% success rate
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINT_QUEUE_ITEM',
          payload: { 
            id: queueItemId, 
            updates: { status: success ? 'completed' : 'failed' } 
          }
        });
        dispatch({
          type: 'UPDATE_PRINTER',
          payload: {
            id: queueItem.printer.id,
            updates: { status: 'online' }
          }
        });
      }, 2000);

      toast.success('Đã gửi lại lệnh in!');
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveItem = (queueItemId) => {
    dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: queueItemId });
    toast.success('Đã xóa khỏi hàng đợi!');
  };

  const handleClearCompleted = () => {
    const completedItems = printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'completed');
    completedItems.forEach(item => {
      dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_PRINT_QUEUE', payload: item.id });
    });
    toast.success(`Đã xóa ${completedItems.length} lệnh in hoàn thành!`);
  };

  const handleClearAll = () => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa toàn bộ hàng đợi in?')) {
      dispatch({ type: 'CLEAR_PRINT_QUEUE' });
      toast.success('Đã xóa toàn bộ hàng đợi in!');
    }
  };

  const getQueueStats = () => {
    const stats = {
      total: printQueue.length,
      printing: printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'printing').length,
      completed: printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'completed').length,
      failed: printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'failed').length,
      pending: printQueue.filter(item => item.status === 'pending').length
    };
    return stats;
  };

  const stats = getQueueStats();

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Hàng đợi in"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="print-queue-modal">
        {/* Stats Summary */}
        <div className="queue-stats">
          <div className="stat-card total">
            <div className="stat-number">{stats.total}</div>
            <div className="stat-label">Tổng cộng</div>
          </div>
          <div className="stat-card printing">
            <div className="stat-number">{stats.printing}</div>
            <div className="stat-label">Đang in</div>
          </div>
          <div className="stat-card completed">
            <div className="stat-number">{stats.completed}</div>
            <div className="stat-label">Hoàn thành</div>
          </div>
          <div className="stat-card failed">
            <div className="stat-number">{stats.failed}</div>
            <div className="stat-label">Thất bại</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Filter and Actions */}
        <div className="queue-controls">
          <div className="filter-tabs">
            <button
              className={`filter-tab ${filter === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setFilter('all')}
            >
              Tất cả ({stats.total})
            </button>
            <button
              className={`filter-tab ${filter === 'printing' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setFilter('printing')}
            >
              Đang in ({stats.printing})
            </button>
            <button
              className={`filter-tab ${filter === 'failed' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setFilter('failed')}
            >
              Thất bại ({stats.failed})
            </button>
            <button
              className={`filter-tab ${filter === 'completed' ? 'active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => setFilter('completed')}
            >
              Hoàn thành ({stats.completed})
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="queue-actions">
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleClearCompleted}
              disabled={stats.completed === 0}
            >
              Xóa hoàn thành
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="error"
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleClearAll}
              disabled={stats.total === 0}
            >
              <Trash2 size={14} />
              Xóa tất cả
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Queue List */}
        <div className="queue-list">
          {filteredQueue.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="queue-empty">
              <Printer size={48} />
              <p>Không có lệnh in nào {filter !== 'all' ? `ở trạng thái "${getStatusText(filter)}"` : 'trong hàng đợi'}</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            filteredQueue.map(item => (
              <div key={item.id} className={`queue-item ${item.status}`}>
                <div className="queue-item-header">
                  <div className="queue-item-info">
                    {getStatusIcon(item.status)}
                    <div className="queue-item-details">
                      <div className="queue-item-title">
                        <span className="queue-type">{item.type === 'kitchen' ? '🍳 Bếp' : item.type === 'bar' ? '🍹 Bar' : '🧾 Hóa đơn'}</span>
                        <span className="queue-table">Bàn {item.table}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="queue-item-meta">
                        <span className="queue-printer">📱 {item.printer.name}</span>
                        <span className="queue-time">⏰ {formatTime(item.createdAt)}</span>
                        <span className="queue-items-count">📋 {item.order.length} món</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="queue-item-actions">
                    {item.status === 'failed' && (
                      <Button
                        variant="warning"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleRetryPrint(item.id)}
                      >
                        <RefreshCw size={14} />
                        Thử lại
                      </Button>
                    )}
                    <Button
                      variant="error"
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveItem(item.id)}
                    >
                      <X size={14} />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Order Items Preview */}
                <div className="queue-item-content">
                  <div className="order-items-preview">
                    {item.order.slice(0, 3).map((orderItem, index) => (
                      <div key={index} className="order-item-preview">
                        <span className="item-name">{orderItem.name}</span>
                        <span className="item-quantity">x{orderItem.quantity}</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    {item.order.length > 3 && (
                      <div className="more-items">
                        +{item.order.length - 3} món khác
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Printer Status */}
        <div className="printer-status-section">
          <h4>Trạng thái máy in</h4>
          <div className="printers-grid">
            {printers.map(printer => (
              <div key={printer.id} className={`printer-status-card ${printer.status}`}>
                <div className="printer-status-header">
                  <Printer size={16} />
                  <span className="printer-name">{printer.name}</span>
                  <span className={`status-badge ${printer.status}`}>
                    {printer.status === 'online' ? 'Sẵn sàng' : 
                     printer.status === 'busy' ? 'Đang in' : 'Offline'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="printer-status-details">
                  <span>📍 {printer.location}</span>
                  <span>🌐 {printer.ip}</span>
                  <span>⏰ {formatTime(printer.lastUsed)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="modal-actions">
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
            Đóng
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default PrintQueueModal;

src/components/Modals/PrintQueueModal.scss
@import '../../styles/variables.scss';
@import '../../styles/mixins.scss';

.print-queue-modal {
  .queue-stats {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, 1fr);
    gap: $spacing-md;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-xl;

    @include responsive(mobile) {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);
    }
  }

  .stat-card {
    padding: $spacing-lg;
    border-radius: $border-radius-lg;
    text-align: center;

    &.total {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #6366f1, #4f46e5);
      color: white;
    }

    &.printing {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #f59e0b, #d97706);
      color: white;
    }

    &.completed {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #22c55e, #16a34a);
      color: white;
    }

    &.failed {
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ef4444, #dc2626);
      color: white;
    }
  }

  .stat-number {
    font-size: $font-size-2xl;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-xs;
  }

  .stat-label {
    font-size: $font-size-sm;
    opacity: 0.9;
  }

  .queue-controls {
    @include flex-between;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-lg;
    padding-bottom: $spacing-lg;
    border-bottom: 1px solid $gray-200;

    @include responsive(mobile) {
      flex-direction: column;
      gap: $spacing-md;
    }
  }

  .filter-tabs {
    display: flex;
    gap: $spacing-xs;
    overflow-x: auto;
  }

  .filter-tab {
    padding: $spacing-sm $spacing-md;
    border: 1px solid $gray-200;
    background: white;
    border-radius: $border-radius-md;
    cursor: pointer;
    font-size: $font-size-sm;
    white-space: nowrap;
    transition: all 0.2s;

    &:hover {
      border-color: $primary-color;
      background: #f0f9ff;
    }

    &.active {
      background: $primary-color;
      color: white;
      border-color: $primary-color;
    }
  }

  .queue-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: $spacing-sm;
  }

  .queue-list {
    max-height: 400px;
    overflow-y: auto;
    @include scrollbar;
    margin-bottom: $spacing-xl;
  }

  .queue-empty {
    text-align: center;
    padding: $spacing-2xl;
    color: $gray-600;

    svg {
      margin-bottom: $spacing-lg;
      color: $gray-400;
    }

    p {
      font-size: $font-size-lg;
      margin: 0;
    }
  }

  .queue-item {
    border: 1px solid $gray-200;
    border-radius: $border-radius-lg;

👥 Staff Management Modal
src/components/Modals/StaffModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Users, Plus, Edit, Trash2, Eye, EyeOff, UserCheck, UserX } from 'lucide-react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import { useLocalStorage } from '../../hooks/useLocalStorage';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './StaffModal.scss';

const StaffModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [staffList, setStaffList] = useLocalStorage('pos-staff', [
    {
      id: 1,
      name: 'Nguyễn Văn Admin',
      username: 'admin',
      role: 'admin',
      phone: '0901234567',
      email: 'admin@foodhub.com',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
      lastLogin: new Date()
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      name: 'Trần Thị Thu Ngân',
      username: 'cashier1',
      role: 'cashier',
      phone: '0987654321',
      email: 'cashier1@foodhub.com',
      status: 'active',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-05'),
      lastLogin: new Date(Date.now() - 3600000)
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      name: 'Lê Văn Phục Vụ',
      username: 'waiter1',
      role: 'waiter',
      phone: '0912345678',
      email: 'waiter1@foodhub.com',
      status: 'inactive',
      createdAt: new Date('2024-01-10'),
      lastLogin: new Date(Date.now() - 86400000)
    }
  ]);

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingStaff, setEditingStaff] = useState(null);
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);

  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm();

  const roles = [
    { value: 'admin', label: 'Quản trị viên', color: '#ef4444' },
    { value: 'manager', label: 'Quản lý', color: '#f59e0b' },
    { value: 'cashier', label: 'Thu ngân', color: '#22c55e' },
    { value: 'waiter', label: 'Phục vụ', color: '#3b82f6' },
    { value: 'kitchen', label: 'Bếp', color: '#8b5cf6' }
  ];

  const getRoleInfo = (roleValue) => {
    return roles.find(role => role.value === roleValue) || roles[0];
  };

  const handleAddStaff = () => {
    setEditingStaff(null);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset();
  };

  const handleEditStaff = (staff) => {
    setEditingStaff(staff);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset({
      name: staff.name,
      username: staff.username,
      role: staff.role,
      phone: staff.phone,
      email: staff.email
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteStaff = (staffId) => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa nhân viên này?')) {
      setStaffList(prev => prev.filter(staff => staff.id !== staffId));
      toast.success('Đã xóa nhân viên!');
    }
  };

  const handleToggleStatus = (staffId) => {
    setStaffList(prev => prev.map(staff => 
      staff.id === staffId 
        ? { ...staff, status: staff.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active' }
        : staff
    ));
    toast.success('Đã cập nhật trạng thái nhân viên!');
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    if (editingStaff) {
      // Update existing staff
      setStaffList(prev => prev.map(staff => 
        staff.id === editingStaff.id 
          ? { ...staff, ...data }
          : staff
      ));
      toast.success('Đã cập nhật thông tin nhân viên!');
    } else {
      // Add new staff
      const newStaff = {
        id: Date.now(),
        ...data,
        status: 'active',
        createdAt: new Date(),
        lastLogin: null
      };
      setStaffList(prev => [...prev, newStaff]);
      toast.success('Đã thêm nhân viên mới!');
    }
    
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingStaff(null);
    reset();
  };

  const handleCloseForm = () => {
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingStaff(null);
    reset();
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Quản lý nhân viên"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="staff-modal">
        {!showForm ? (
          <>
            {/* Staff List Header */}
            <div className="staff-header">
              <div className="staff-stats">
                <div className="stat-item">
                  <span className="stat-number">{staffList.length}</span>
                  <span className="stat-label">Tổng nhân viên</span>
                </div>
                <div className="stat-item">
                  <span className="stat-number">{staffList.filter(s => s.status === 'active').length}</span>
                  <span className="stat-label">Đang hoạt động</span>
                </div>
                <div className="stat-item">
                  <span className="stat-number">{staffList.filter(s => s.status === 'inactive').length}</span>
                  <span className="stat-label">Tạm nghỉ</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleAddStaff}>
                <Plus size={16} />
                Thêm nhân viên
              </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Staff List */}
            <div className="staff-list">
              {staffList.map(staff => {
                const roleInfo = getRoleInfo(staff.role);
                return (
                  <div key={staff.id} className={`staff-card ${staff.status}`}>
                    <div className="staff-info">
                      <div className="staff-avatar">
                        <Users size={24} />
                      </div>
                      
                      <div className="staff-details">
                        <div className="staff-name">{staff.name}</div>
                        <div className="staff-meta">
                          <span 
                            className="staff-role"
                            style={{ backgroundColor: roleInfo.color }}
                          >
                            {roleInfo.label}
                          </span>
                          <span className="staff-username">@{staff.username}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="staff-contact">
                          <span>📞 {staff.phone}</span>
                          <span>📧 {staff.email}</span>
                        </div>
                        {staff.lastLogin && (
                          <div className="staff-last-login">
                            Đăng nhập cuối: {staff.lastLogin.toLocaleString('vi-VN')}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="staff-actions">
                      <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleStatus(staff.id)}
                        title={staff.status === 'active' ? 'Tạm nghỉ' : 'Kích hoạt'}
                      >
                        {staff.status === 'active' ? <UserX size={14} /> : <UserCheck size={14} />}
                      </Button>
                      
                      <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleEditStaff(staff)}
                        title="Chỉnh sửa"
                      >
                        <Edit size={14} />
                      </Button>
                      
                      <Button
                        variant="error"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteStaff(staff.id)}
                        title="Xóa"
                        disabled={staff.role === 'admin'}
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={14} />
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* Staff Form */
          <div className="staff-form">
            <div className="form-header">
              <h3>{editingStaff ? 'Chỉnh sửa nhân viên' : 'Thêm nhân viên mới'}</h3>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
              <div className="form-grid">
                <Input
                  label="Họ và tên:"
                  {...register('name', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập họ tên' })}
                  error={errors.name?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập họ và tên"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tên đăng nhập:"
                  {...register('username', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tên đăng nhập' })}
                  error={errors.username?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập tên đăng nhập"
                />

                <div className="form-group">
                  <label className="form-label">Vai trò:</label>
                  <select
                    {...register('role', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn vai trò' })}
                    className="input"
                  >
                    <option value="">Chọn vai trò</option>
                    {roles.map(role => (
                      <option key={role.value} value={role.value}>
                        {role.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  {errors.role && (
                    <span className="input-error">{errors.role.message}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Input
                  label="Số điện thoại:"
                  type="tel"
                  {...register('phone', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập số điện thoại' })}
                  error={errors.phone?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập số điện thoại"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Email:"
                  type="email"
                  {...register('email', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập email' })}
                  error={errors.email?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập địa chỉ email"
                />

                {!editingStaff && (
                  <div className="form-group">
                    <label className="form-label">Mật khẩu:</label>
                    <div className="password-input">
                      <input
                        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        {...register('password', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu' })}
                        className="input"
                        placeholder="Nhập mật khẩu"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="password-toggle"
                        onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                      >
                        {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    {errors.password && (
                      <span className="input-error">{errors.password.message}</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="form-actions">
                <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
                  {editingStaff ? 'Cập nhật' : 'Thêm nhân viên'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {!showForm && (
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Đóng
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default StaffModal;

📦 Inventory Modal
src/components/Modals/InventoryModal.jsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Package, Plus, Edit, Trash2, AlertTriangle, TrendingDown, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import { useLocalStorage } from '../../hooks/useLocalStorage';
import { formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './InventoryModal.scss';

const InventoryModal = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useLocalStorage('pos-inventory', [
    {
      id: 1,
      name: 'Thịt bò thăn',
      category: 'meat',
      unit: 'kg',
      currentStock: 15,
      minStock: 5,
      maxStock: 50,
      costPrice: 450000,
      supplier: 'Công ty Thực phẩm ABC',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'in_stock'
    },
    {
      id: 2,
      name: 'Tôm hùm',
      category: 'seafood',
      unit: 'kg',
      currentStock: 3,
      minStock: 5,
      maxStock: 20,
      costPrice: 1200000,
      supplier: 'Hải sản Tươi Sống',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'low_stock'
    },
    {
      id: 3,
      name: 'Rau xà lách',
      category: 'vegetable',
      unit: 'kg',
      currentStock: 0,
      minStock: 2,
      maxStock: 10,
      costPrice: 25000,
      supplier: 'Nông trại Xanh',
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      status: 'out_of_stock'
    }
  ]);

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState('all');

  const { register, handleSubmit, reset, formState: { errors } } = useForm();

  const categories = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'Tất cả' },
    { value: 'meat', label: 'Thịt' },
    { value: 'seafood', label: 'Hải sản' },
    { value: 'vegetable', label: 'Rau củ' },
    { value: 'spice', label: 'Gia vị' },
    { value: 'beverage', label: 'Đồ uống' },
    { value: 'other', label: 'Khác' }
  ];

  const getStatusInfo = (item) => {
    if (item.currentStock === 0) {
      return { status: 'out_of_stock', label: 'Hết hàng', color: '#ef4444' };
    } else if (item.currentStock <= item.minStock) {
      return { status: 'low_stock', label: 'Sắp hết', color: '#f59e0b' };
    } else {
      return { status: 'in_stock', label: 'Còn hàng', color: '#22c55e' };
    }
  };

  const filteredInventory = inventory.filter(item => {
    const matchesSearch = !searchTerm || 
      item.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      item.supplier.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());
    
    const matchesCategory = filterCategory === 'all' || item.category === filterCategory;
    
    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory;
  });

  const handleAddItem = () => {
    setEditingItem(null);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset();
  };

  const handleEditItem = (item) => {
    setEditingItem(item);
    setShowForm(true);
    reset({
      name: item.name,
      category: item.category,
      unit: item.unit,
      currentStock: item.currentStock,
      minStock: item.minStock,
      maxStock: item.maxStock,
      costPrice: item.costPrice,
      supplier: item.supplier
    });
  };

  const handleDeleteItem = (itemId) => {
    if (window.confirm('Bạn có chắc muốn xóa mặt hàng này?')) {
      setInventory(prev => prev.filter(item => item.id !== itemId));
      toast.success('Đã xóa mặt hàng!');
    }
  };

  const handleUpdateStock = (itemId, newStock) => {
    setInventory(prev => prev.map(item => 
      item.id === itemId 
        ? { 
            ...item, 
            currentStock: Math.max(0, newStock),
            lastUpdated: new Date()
          }
        : item
    ));
  };

  const onSubmit = (data) => {
    const processedData = {
      ...data,
      currentStock: parseFloat(data.currentStock),
      minStock: parseFloat(data.minStock),
      maxStock: parseFloat(data.maxStock),
      costPrice: parseFloat(data.costPrice)
    };

    if (editingItem) {
      setInventory(prev => prev.map(item => 
        item.id === editingItem.id 
          ? { ...item, ...processedData, lastUpdated: new Date() }
          : item
      ));
      toast.success('Đã cập nhật mặt hàng!');
    } else {
      const newItem = {
        id: Date.now(),
        ...processedData,
        lastUpdated: new Date()
      };
      setInventory(prev => [...prev, newItem]);
      toast.success('Đã thêm mặt hàng mới!');
    }
    
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingItem(null);
    reset();
  };

  const handleCloseForm = () => {
    setShowForm(false);
    setEditingItem(null);
    reset();
  };

  const getInventoryStats = () => {
    const total = inventory.length;
    const inStock = inventory.filter(item => getStatusInfo(item).status === 'in_stock').length;
    const lowStock = inventory.filter(item => getStatusInfo(item).status === 'low_stock').length;
    const outOfStock = inventory.filter(item => getStatusInfo(item).status === 'out_of_stock').length;
    const totalValue = inventory.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.currentStock * item.costPrice), 0);

    return { total, inStock, lowStock, outOfStock, totalValue };
  };

  const stats = getInventoryStats();

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title="Quản lý kho hàng"
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="inventory-modal">
        {!showForm ? (
          <>
            {/* Inventory Stats */}
            <div className="inventory-stats">
              <div className="stat-card total">
                <div className="stat-icon">
                  <Package size={24} />
                </div>
                <div className="stat-content">
                  <div className="stat-number">{stats.total}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Tổng mặt hàng</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="stat-card in-stock">
                <div className="stat-icon">
                  <Package size={24} />
                </div>
                <div className="stat-content">
                  <div className="stat-number">{stats.inStock}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Còn hàng</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="stat-card low-stock">
                <div className="stat-icon">
                  <TrendingDown size={24} />
                </div>
                <div className="stat-content">
                  <div className="stat-number">{stats.lowStock}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Sắp hết</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="stat-card out-of-stock">
                <div className="stat-icon">
                  <AlertTriangle size={24} />
                </div>
                <div className="stat-content">
                  <div className="stat-number">{stats.outOfStock}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Hết hàng</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="stat-card value">
                <div className="stat-content">
                  <div className="stat-number">{formatPrice(stats.totalValue)}</div>
                  <div className="stat-label">Giá trị kho</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Controls */}
            <div className="inventory-controls">
              <div className="search-and-filter">
                <div className="search-box">
                  <Search size={16} />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Tìm kiếm mặt hàng..."
                    value={searchTerm}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                    className="search-input"
                  />
                </div>

                <select
                  value={filterCategory}
                  onChange={(e) => setFilterCategory(e.target.value)}
                  className="category-filter"
                >
                  {categories.map(category => (
                    <option key={category.value} value={category.value}>
                      {category.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleAddItem}>
                <Plus size={16} />
                Thêm mặt hàng
              </Button>
            </div>

            {/* Inventory List */}
            <div className="inventory-list">
              {filteredInventory.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="inventory-empty">
                  <Package size={48} />
                  <p>Không tìm thấy mặt hàng nào</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                filteredInventory.map(item => {
                  const statusInfo = getStatusInfo(item);
                  return (
                    <div key={item.id} className={`inventory-item ${statusInfo.status}`}>
                      <div className="item-info">
                        <div className="item-header">
                          <div className="item-name">{item.name}</div>
                          <div 
                            className="item-status"
                            style={{ backgroundColor: statusInfo.color }}
                          >
                            {statusInfo.label}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="item-details">
                          <div className="item-stock">
                            <span className="stock-current">{item.currentStock}</span>
                            <span className="stock-unit">/{item.unit}</span>
                            <span className="stock-range">
                              (Min: {item.minStock}, Max: {item.maxStock})
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          
                          <div className="item-meta">
                            <span>💰 {formatPrice(item.costPrice)}/{item.unit}</span>
                            <span>🏪 {item.supplier}</span>
                            <span>📅 {item.lastUpdated.toLocaleDateString('vi-VN')}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="stock-progress">
                          <div 
                            className="progress-bar"
                            style={{ 
                              width: `${Math.min((item.currentStock / item.maxStock) * 100, 100)}%`,
                              backgroundColor: statusInfo.color
                            }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="item-actions">
                        <div className="stock-controls">
                          <button
                            className="stock-btn decrease"
                            onClick={() => handleUpdateStock(item.id, item.currentStock - 1)}
                            disabled={item.currentStock <= 0}
                          >
                            -
                          </button>
                          <span className="stock-display">{item.currentStock}</span>
                          <button
                            className="stock-btn increase"
                            onClick={() => handleUpdateStock(item.id, item.currentStock + 1)}
                          >
                            +
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="action-buttons">
                          <Button
                            variant="secondary"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleEditItem(item)}
                          >
                            <Edit size={14} />
                          </Button>
                          
                          <Button
                            variant="error"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => handleDeleteItem(item.id)}
                          >
                            <Trash2 size={14} />
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* Inventory Form */
          <div className="inventory-form">
            <div className="form-header">
              <h3>{editingItem ? 'Chỉnh sửa mặt hàng' : 'Thêm mặt hàng mới'}</h3>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
              <div className="form-grid">
                <Input
                  label="Tên mặt hàng:"
                  {...register('name', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tên mặt hàng' })}
                  error={errors.name?.message}
                  placeholder="Nhập tên mặt hàng"
                />

                <div className="form-group">
                  <label className="form-label">Danh mục:</label>
                  <select
                    {...register('category', { required: 'Vui lòng chọn danh mục' })}
                    className="input"
                  >
                    <option value="">Chọn danh mục</option>
                    {categories.slice(1).map(category => (
                      <option key={category.value} value={category.value}>
                        {category.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                  {errors.category && (
                    <span className="input-error">{errors.category.message}</span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <Input
                  label="Đơn vị tính:"
                  {...register('unit', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập đơn vị tính' })}
                  error={errors.unit?.message}
                  placeholder="kg, lít, hộp..."
                />

                <Input
                  label="Số lượng hiện tại:"
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  {...register('currentStock', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập số lượng' })}
                  error={errors.currentStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tồn kho tối thiểu:"
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  {...register('minStock', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tồn kho tối thiểu' })}
                  error={errors.minStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Tồn kho tối đa:"
                  type="number"
                  step="0.1"
                  {...register('maxStock', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập tồn kho tối đa' })}
                  error={errors.maxStock?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Giá nhập (VNĐ):"
                  type="number"
                  {...register('costPrice', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập giá nhập' })}
                  error={errors.costPrice?.message}
                  placeholder="0"
                />

                <Input
                  label="Nhà cung cấp:"
                  {...register('supplier', { required: 'Vui lòng nhập nhà cung cấp' })}
                  error={errors.supplier?.message}
                  placeholder="Tên nhà cung cấp"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="form-actions">
                <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={handleCloseForm}>
                  Hủy
                </Button>
                <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
                  {editingItem ? 'Cập nhật' : 'Thêm mặt hàng'}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        )}

        {!showForm && (
          <div className="modal-actions">
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Đóng
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default InventoryModal;

src/components/Modals/InventoryModal.scss

🔄 Updated App.jsx với tất cả Modal
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Toaster } from 'react-hot-toast';
import { POSProvider } from './context/POSContext';
import { useKeyboard } from './hooks/useKeyboard';
import TableGrid from './components/Tables/TableGrid';
import MenuGrid from './components/Menu/MenuGrid';
import OrderPanel from './components/Order/OrderPanel';
import MobileMenu from './components/Layout/MobileMenu';

// All Modals
import MenuItemModal from './components/Modals/MenuItemModal';
import PaymentModal from './components/Modals/PaymentModal';
import PrintModal from './components/Modals/PrintModal';
import PrintQueueModal from './components/Modals/PrintQueueModal';
import ReservationModal from './components/Modals/ReservationModal';
import TableInfoModal from './components/Modals/TableInfoModal';
import MergeTableModal from './components/Modals/MergeTableModal';
import MoveTableModal from './components/Modals/MoveTableModal';
import SettingsModal from './components/Settings/SettingsModal';
import ReportsModal from './components/Reports/ReportsModal';
import StaffModal from './components/Modals/StaffModal';
import InventoryModal from './components/Modals/InventoryModal';

import './styles/globals.scss';

function App() {
  // Modal states
  const [showMenuItemModal, setShowMenuItemModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPaymentModal, setShowPaymentModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPrintModal, setShowPrintModal] = useState(false);
  const [showPrintQueueModal, setShowPrintQueueModal] = useState(false);
  const [showReservationModal, setShowReservationModal] = useState(false);
  const [showTableInfoModal, setShowTableInfoModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMergeTableModal, setShowMergeTableModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMoveTableModal, setShowMoveTableModal] = useState(false);
  const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showReportsModal, setShowReportsModal] = useState(false);
  const [showStaffModal, setShowStaffModal] = useState(false);
  const [showInventoryModal, setShowInventoryModal] = useState(false);

  // Data states
  const [selectedMenuItem, setSelectedMenuItem] = useState(null);
  const [selectedTableCode, setSelectedTableCode] = useState(null);
  const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('tables');

  // Keyboard shortcuts
  useKeyboard({
    'ctrl+s': () => console.log('Save shortcut'),
    'ctrl+p': () => setShowPrintModal(true),
    'ctrl+shift+p': () => setShowPrintQueueModal(true),
    'ctrl+n': () => setShowReservationModal(true),
    'ctrl+r': () => setShowReportsModal(true),
    'ctrl+u': () => setShowStaffModal(true),
    'ctrl+i': () => setShowInventoryModal(true),
    'f1': () => setShowSettingsModal(true),
    'f2': () => setShowReportsModal(true),
    'escape': () => {
      // Close any open modal
      setShowMenuItemModal(false);
      setShowPaymentModal(false);
      setShowPrintModal(false);
      setShowPrintQueueModal(false);
      setShowReservationModal(false);
      setShowTableInfoModal(false);
      setShowMergeTableModal(false);
      setShowMoveTableModal(false);
      setShowSettingsModal(false);
      setShowReportsModal(false);
      setShowStaffModal(false);
      setShowInventoryModal(false);
    }
  });

  const handleMenuItemClick = (menuItem) => {
    setSelectedMenuItem(menuItem);
    setShowMenuItemModal(true);
  };

  const handleCloseMenuItemModal = () => {
    setShowMenuItemModal(false);
    setSelectedMenuItem(null);
  };

  const handleTableInfo = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowTableInfoModal(true);
  };

  const handleMergeTable = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowMergeTableModal(true);
  };

  const handleMoveTable = (tableCode) => {
    setSelectedTableCode(tableCode);
    setShowMoveTableModal(true);
  };

  return (
    <POSProvider>
      <div className="pos-container">
        <MobileMenu 
          currentView={currentView} 
          onViewChange={setCurrentView} 
        />

        <div className="left-panel">
          <TableGrid 
            onTableInfo={handleTableInfo}
            onMergeTable={handleMergeTable}
            onMoveTable={handleMoveTable}
            onShowPrintQueue={() => setShowPrintQueueModal(true)}
          />
        </div>

        <div className="center-panel">
          <MenuGrid onMenuItemClick={handleMenuItemClick} />
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <OrderPanel 
            onShowPrintModal={() => setShowPrintModal(true)}
            onProcessPayment={() => setShowPaymentModal(true)}
          />
        </div>

        {/* All Modals */}
        <MenuItemModal
          isOpen={showMenuItemModal}
          onClose={handleCloseMenuItemModal}
          menuItem={selectedMenuItem}
        />

        <PaymentModal
          isOpen={showPaymentModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPaymentModal(false)}
        />

        <PrintModal
          isOpen={showPrintModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPrintModal(false)}
        />

        <PrintQueueModal
          isOpen={showPrintQueueModal}
          onClose={() => setShowPrintQueueModal(false)}
        />

        <ReservationModal
          isOpen={showReservationModal}
          onClose={() => setShowReservationModal(false)}
        />

        <TableInfoModal
          isOpen={showTableInfoModal}
          onClose={() => setShowTableInfoModal(false)}
          tableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <MergeTableModal
          isOpen={showMergeTableModal}
          onClose={() => setShowMergeTableModal(false)}
          sourceTableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <MoveTableModal
          isOpen={showMoveTableModal}
          onClose={() => setShowMoveTableModal(false)}
          sourceTableCode={selectedTableCode}
        />

        <SettingsModal
          isOpen={showSettingsModal}
          onClose={() => setShowSettingsModal(false)}
        />

        <ReportsModal
          isOpen={showReportsModal}
          onClose={() => setShowReportsModal(false)}
        />

        <StaffModal
          isOpen={showStaffModal}
          onClose={() => setShowStaffModal(false)}
        />

        <InventoryModal
          isOpen={showInventoryModal}
          onClose={() => setShowInventoryModal(false)}
        />

        <Toaster 
          position="top-right"
          toastOptions={{
            duration: 4000,
            style: {
              background: '#363636',
              color: '#fff',
            },
            success: {
              style: {
                background: '#22c55e',
              },
            },
            error: {
              style: {
                background: '#ef4444',
              },
            },
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </POSProvider>
  );
}

export default App;

🎯 Tổng kết HOÀN CHỈNH:
✅ Tất cả Modal đã có:
MenuItemModal - Thêm món vào đơn hàng
PaymentModal - Thanh toán với nhiều phương thức
PrintModal - Quản lý in ấn
PrintQueueModal - Hàng đợi in chi tiết
ReservationModal - Đặt bàn
TableInfoModal - Thông tin chi tiết bàn
MergeTableModal - Gộp bàn
MoveTableModal - Chuyển bàn
SettingsModal - Cài đặt hệ thống
ReportsModal - Báo cáo doanh thu
StaffModal - Quản lý nhân viên
InventoryModal - Quản lý kho hàng
✅ Keyboard Shortcuts:
Ctrl+S: Lưu
Ctrl+P: In đơn
Ctrl+Shift+P: Hàng đợi in
Ctrl+N: Đặt bàn mới
Ctrl+R: Báo cáo
Ctrl+U: Quản lý nhân viên
Ctrl+I: Quản lý kho
F1: Cài đặt
ESC: Đóng modal
Bây giờ hệ thống POS đã HOÀN TOÀN ĐẦY ĐỦ với tất cả tính năng cần thiết cho một nhà hàng thực tế! 🚀
