còn thiếu SplitBillModal.jsx và SplitBillModal.scss

Bạn đúng rồi! Tôi thiếu SplitBillModal - một tính năng quan trọng để chia hóa đơn. Để tôi bổ sung ngay:

Bạn đúng rồi! Tôi thiếu SplitBillModal. Để tôi bổ sung ngay:

💰 Split Bill Modal
src/components/Modals/SplitBillModal.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Users, Plus, Minus, Trash2, Calculator, DollarSign, Check, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePOS } from '../../context/POSContext';
import { formatPrice } from '../../utils/formatters';
import Modal from '../Common/Modal';
import Button from '../Common/Button';
import Input from '../Common/Input';
import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
import './SplitBillModal.scss';

const SplitBillModal = ({ isOpen, onClose, tableCode }) => {
  const { state, dispatch } = usePOS();
  const [splitMethod, setSplitMethod] = useState('equal'); // equal, custom, items
  const [numberOfPeople, setNumberOfPeople] = useState(2);
  const [customAmounts, setCustomAmounts] = useState([]);
  const [itemAssignments, setItemAssignments] = useState({});
  const [people, setPeople] = useState([]);
  const [showPaymentOptions, setShowPaymentOptions] = useState(false);

  const currentTable = state.tables[tableCode];
  const currentOrder = currentTable?.currentOrder || { items: [], total: 0 };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen && currentOrder.items.length > 0) {
      initializeSplit();
    }
  }, [isOpen, currentOrder]);

  const initializeSplit = () => {
    // Initialize people
    const initialPeople = Array.from({ length: numberOfPeople }, (_, index) => ({
      id: index + 1,
      name: `Người ${index + 1}`,
      amount: 0,
      items: [],
      paymentMethod: 'cash'
    }));
    setPeople(initialPeople);

    // Initialize custom amounts
    const equalAmount = Math.round(currentOrder.total / numberOfPeople);
    setCustomAmounts(Array(numberOfPeople).fill(equalAmount));

    // Initialize item assignments
    const assignments = {};
    currentOrder.items.forEach(item => {
      assignments[item.id] = Array(numberOfPeople).fill(false);
    });
    setItemAssignments(assignments);

    calculateSplit();
  };

  const calculateSplit = () => {
    let updatedPeople = [...people];

    switch (splitMethod) {
      case 'equal':
        const equalAmount = Math.round(currentOrder.total / numberOfPeople);
        updatedPeople = updatedPeople.map(person => ({
          ...person,
          amount: equalAmount,
          items: [...currentOrder.items]
        }));
        break;

      case 'custom':
        updatedPeople = updatedPeople.map((person, index) => ({
          ...person,
          amount: customAmounts[index] || 0,
          items: [...currentOrder.items]
        }));
        break;

      case 'items':
        updatedPeople = updatedPeople.map((person, index) => {
          const assignedItems = currentOrder.items.filter(item => 
            itemAssignments[item.id] && itemAssignments[item.id][index]
          );
          const totalAmount = assignedItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.total, 0);
          
          return {
            ...person,
            amount: totalAmount,
            items: assignedItems
          };
        });
        break;
    }

    setPeople(updatedPeople);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (people.length > 0) {
      calculateSplit();
    }
  }, [splitMethod, numberOfPeople, customAmounts, itemAssignments]);

  const handleNumberOfPeopleChange = (newNumber) => {
    const validNumber = Math.max(2, Math.min(10, newNumber));
    setNumberOfPeople(validNumber);
    
    // Adjust arrays
    const newCustomAmounts = [...customAmounts];
    const newPeople = [...people];
    
    if (validNumber > customAmounts.length) {
      // Add new people
      const equalAmount = Math.round(currentOrder.total / validNumber);
      for (let i = customAmounts.length; i < validNumber; i++) {
        newCustomAmounts.push(equalAmount);
        newPeople.push({
          id: i + 1,
          name: `Người ${i + 1}`,
          amount: 0,
          items: [],
          paymentMethod: 'cash'
        });
      }
    } else {
      // Remove people
      newCustomAmounts.splice(validNumber);
      newPeople.splice(validNumber);
    }
    
    setCustomAmounts(newCustomAmounts);
    setPeople(newPeople);

    // Update item assignments
    const newAssignments = { ...itemAssignments };
    Object.keys(newAssignments).forEach(itemId => {
      if (validNumber > newAssignments[itemId].length) {
        // Add assignments for new people
        for (let i = newAssignments[itemId].length; i < validNumber; i++) {
          newAssignments[itemId].push(false);
        }
      } else {
        // Remove assignments for removed people
        newAssignments[itemId].splice(validNumber);
      }
    });
    setItemAssignments(newAssignments);
  };

  const handleCustomAmountChange = (index, amount) => {
    const newAmounts = [...customAmounts];
    newAmounts[index] = parseFloat(amount) || 0;
    setCustomAmounts(newAmounts);
  };

  const handleItemAssignment = (itemId, personIndex) => {
    const newAssignments = { ...itemAssignments };
    newAssignments[itemId][personIndex] = !newAssignments[itemId][personIndex];
    setItemAssignments(newAssignments);
  };

  const handlePersonNameChange = (index, name) => {
    const newPeople = [...people];
    newPeople[index].name = name;
    setPeople(newPeople);
  };

  const handlePaymentMethodChange = (index, method) => {
    const newPeople = [...people];
    newPeople[index].paymentMethod = method;
    setPeople(newPeople);
  };

  const getTotalAssigned = () => {
    return people.reduce((sum, person) => sum + person.amount, 0);
  };

  const getRemainingAmount = () => {
    return currentOrder.total - getTotalAssigned();
  };

  const isValidSplit = () => {
    const totalAssigned = getTotalAssigned();
    const remaining = Math.abs(currentOrder.total - totalAssigned);
    return remaining < 1000; // Allow 1000 VND difference for rounding
  };

  const handleProcessSplit = () => {
    if (!isValidSplit()) {
      toast.error('Tổng tiền chia không khớp với hóa đơn!');
      return;
    }

    // Create separate bills for each person
    const splitBills = people.map(person => ({
      id: `${tableCode}-${person.id}-${Date.now()}`,
      tableCode,
      personName: person.name,
      items: person.items,
      total: person.amount,
      paymentMethod: person.paymentMethod,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      status: 'pending'
    }));

    // Update table state
    dispatch({
      type: 'SPLIT_BILL',
      payload: {
        tableCode,
        splitBills
      }
    });

    toast.success(`Đã chia hóa đơn thành ${people.length} phần!`);
    setShowPaymentOptions(true);
  };

  const handlePayBill = (billIndex) => {
    const bill = people[billIndex];
    
    // Process payment
    dispatch({
      type: 'PROCESS_SPLIT_PAYMENT',
      payload: {
        tableCode,
        billId: `${tableCode}-${bill.id}`,
        amount: bill.amount,
        paymentMethod: bill.paymentMethod
      }
    });

    toast.success(`Đã thanh toán cho ${bill.name}: ${formatPrice(bill.amount)}`);
    
    // Update person status
    const newPeople = [...people];
    newPeople[billIndex].status = 'paid';
    setPeople(newPeople);
  };

  const handleFinishSplit = () => {
    const unpaidBills = people.filter(person => person.status !== 'paid');
    
    if (unpaidBills.length > 0) {
      if (!window.confirm(`Còn ${unpaidBills.length} hóa đơn chưa thanh toán. Bạn có muốn tiếp tục?`)) {
        return;
      }
    }

    onClose();
    toast.success('Hoàn thành chia hóa đơn!');
  };

  if (!currentOrder.items.length) {
    return (
      <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose} title="Chia hóa đơn" size="md">
        <div className="split-bill-modal">
          <div className="empty-order">
            <Calculator size={48} />
            <h3>Không có đơn hàng</h3>
            <p>Bàn {tableCode} chưa có đơn hàng để chia.</p>
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={onClose}>
              Đóng
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Modal
      isOpen={isOpen}
      onClose={onClose}
      title={`Chia hóa đơn - Bàn ${tableCode}`}
      size="xl"
    >
      <div className="split-bill-modal">
        {!showPaymentOptions ? (
          <>
            {/* Order Summary */}
            <div className="order-summary">
              <div className="summary-header">
                <h3>Thông tin hóa đơn</h3>
                <div className="total-amount">{formatPrice(currentOrder.total)}</div>
              </div>
              <div className="order-items">
                {currentOrder.items.map(item => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="order-item">
                    <span className="item-name">{item.name}</span>
                    <span className="item-quantity">x{item.quantity}</span>
                    <span className="item-price">{formatPrice(item.total)}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Split Method Selection */}
            <div className="split-methods">
              <h3>Phương thức chia</h3>
              <div className="method-options">
                <label className={`method-option ${splitMethod === 'equal' ? 'active' : ''}`}>
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    value="equal"
                    checked={splitMethod === 'equal'}
                    onChange={(e) => setSplitMethod(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <div className="option-content">
                    <Users size={20} />
                    <div>
                      <div className="option-title">Chia đều</div>
                      <div className="option-description">Chia đều cho tất cả mọi người</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className={`method-option ${splitMethod === 'custom' ? 'active' : ''}`}>
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    value="custom"
                    checked={splitMethod === 'custom'}
                    onChange={(e) => setSplitMethod(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <div className="option-content">
                    <Calculator size={20} />
                    <div>
                      <div className="option-title">Tùy chỉnh</div>
                      <div className="option-description">Nhập số tiền cho từng người</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className={`method-option ${splitMethod === 'items' ? 'active' : ''}`}>
                  <input
                    type="radio"
                    value="items"
                    checked={splitMethod === 'items'}
                    onChange={(e) => setSplitMethod(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <div className="option-content">
                    <DollarSign size={20} />
                    <div>
                      <div className="option-title">Theo món</div>
                      <div className="option-description">Phân chia theo từng món ăn</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </label>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Number of People */}
            <div className="people-selector">
              <h3>Số người</h3>
              <div className="people-controls">
                <button
                  className="people-btn"
                  onClick={() => handleNumberOfPeopleChange(numberOfPeople - 1)}
                  disabled={numberOfPeople <= 2}
                >
                  <Minus size={16} />
                </button>
                <span className="people-count">{numberOfPeople} người</span>
                <button
                  className="people-btn"
                  onClick={() => handleNumberOfPeopleChange(numberOfPeople + 1)}
                  disabled={numberOfPeople >= 10}
                >
                  <Plus size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Split Configuration */}
            <div className="split-configuration">
              {splitMethod === 'custom' && (
                <div className="custom-amounts">
                  <h3>Nhập số tiền cho từng người</h3>
                  <div className="amounts-grid">
                    {people.map((person, index) => (
                      <div key={person.id} className="amount-input">
                        <Input
                          label={`${person.name}:`}
                          type="number"
                          value={customAmounts[index]}
                          onChange={(e) => handleCustomAmountChange(index, e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="0"
                        />
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {splitMethod === 'items' && (
                <div className="item-assignments">
                  <h3>Phân chia theo món</h3>
                  <div className="assignments-table">
                    <div className="table-header">
                      <div className="item-column">Món ăn</div>
                      {people.map(person => (
                        <div key={person.id} className="person-column">
                          <input
                            type="text"
                            value={person.name}
                            onChange={(e) => handlePersonNameChange(person.id - 1, e.target.value)}
                            className="person-name-input"
                          />
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    
                    {currentOrder.items.map(item => (
                      <div key={item.id} className="assignment-row">
                        <div className="item-info">
                          <div className="item-name">{item.name}</div>
                          <div className="item-price">{formatPrice(item.total)}</div>
                        </div>
                        {people.map((person, personIndex) => (
                          <div key={person.id} className="assignment-cell">
                            <input
                              type="checkbox"
                              checked={itemAssignments[item.id]?.[personIndex] || false}
                              onChange={() => handleItemAssignment(item.id, personIndex)}
                            />
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Split Summary */}
            <div className="split-summary">
              <h3>Tóm tắt chia hóa đơn</h3>
              <div className="summary-grid">
                {people.map((person, index) => (
                  <div key={person.id} className="person-summary">
                    <div className="person-header">
                      <span className="person-name">{person.name}</span>
                      <span className="person-amount">{formatPrice(person.amount)}</span>
                    </div>
                    {splitMethod === 'items' && (
                      <div className="person-items">
                        {person.items.map(item => (
                          <div key={item.id} className="person-item">
                            {item.name} x{item.quantity}
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              
              <div className="summary-totals">
                <div className="total-row">
                  <span>Tổng đã chia:</span>
                  <span>{formatPrice(getTotalAssigned())}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="total-row">
                  <span>Tổng hóa đơn:</span>
                  <span>{formatPrice(currentOrder.total)}</span>
                </div>
                <div className={`total-row ${getRemainingAmount() !== 0 ? 'error' : 'success'}`}>
                  <span>Chênh lệch:</span>
                  <span>{formatPrice(getRemainingAmount())}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Actions */}
            <div className="modal-actions">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
                Hủy
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="primary"
                onClick={handleProcessSplit}
                disabled={!isValidSplit()}
              >
                Chia hóa đơn
              </Button>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          /* Payment Options */
          <div className="payment-options">
            <h3>Thanh toán từng phần</h3>
            <div className="payment-grid">
              {people.map((person, index) => (
                <div key={person.id} className={`payment-card ${person.status === 'paid' ? 'paid' : ''}`}>
                  <div className="payment-header">
                    <span className="person-name">{person.name}</span>
                    <span className="person-amount">{formatPrice(person.amount)}</span>
                    {person.status === 'paid' && (
                      <Check className="paid-icon" size={20} />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  
                  {person.status !== 'paid' && (
                    <div className="payment-controls">
                      <select
                        value={person.paymentMethod}
                        onChange={(e) => handlePaymentMethodChange(index, e.target.value)}
                        className="payment-method-select"
                      >
                        <option value="cash">Tiền mặt</option>
                        <option value="card">Thẻ</option>
                        <option value="transfer">Chuyển khoản</option>
                        <option value="ewallet">Ví điện tử</option>
                      </select>
                      
                      <Button
                        variant="success"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => handlePayBill(index)}
                      >
                        Thanh toán
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="payment-actions">
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setShowPaymentOptions(false)}>
                Quay lại
              </Button>
              <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleFinishSplit}>
                Hoàn thành
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
};

export default SplitBillModal;

    }

    .payment-card {

      &.paid {

        background: #f0fdf4;
      }

        @include flex-between;
        margin-bottom: $spacing-md;
        align-items: center;

        .person-name {
          font-weight: 600;

          font-size: $font-size-lg;

    .payment-actions {
      display: flex;

      justify-content: center;
      border-top: 1px solid $gray-200;
      padding-top: $spacing-lg;
    }
  }

  .modal-actions {
    display: flex;
    gap: $spacing-md;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    border-top: 1px solid $gray-200;
    padding-top: $spacing-lg;
  }

  // Responsive Design
  @include responsive(mobile) {
    .split-methods .method-options {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .split-configuration {
      .custom-amounts .amounts-grid {
        grid-template-columns: 1fr;
      }

      .item-assignments {
        .table-header,
        .assignment-row {
          grid-template-columns: 1fr;
        }

        .assignment-cell {
          justify-content: flex-start;
          gap: $spacing-sm;

          &::before {
            content: attr(data-person);
            font-size: $font-size-sm;
            color: $gray-600;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    .split-summary .summary-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .payment-options .payment-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .modal-actions,
    .payment-actions {
      flex-direction: column;

      .btn {
        width: 100%;
      }
    }
  }
}
